# Life of the Ethiopian Monk Malke‘a Krestos (Vita del santo monaco etiopico)

**Author**: Anonymous  
**Series**: PO Tome 51  
**Columns**: pp. 272–380  

[p. 272] {#po.51.p272}

### THE GADL OF MALKE'A KRESTOS
ገድለ ፡መልክከዐ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።
Prologue
" በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳ አምላከ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ መንግ ሥት ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ ሥልጣን ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ ምዥናን ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ ሥምረት ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ ቅድምናሆሙ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፡ ኅሩያን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ በቅድምና ።፡፡ ኢሀሎ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘመነ ፡ ወኢአሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሾ ስም ፡ ፳ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሊሉያን ፡ አሙንቱ ፡ በአካ ላት ፳ ወኅዱራን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ተሌልዮ ። ወይትባረከ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይትባረክ ፡ ዝከሩ ። ወፍጽምናሁ ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ሣህሱ ፡ ወምሕረቱ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፣፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ንንግር ፡ ወንዜኑ ፡ ጥንተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ንጹሕ ፥፡ ወኅሩይ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ስም ፡ ወሠናየ ፡ ዜና ፡ ትሩፈ ፡ ገድል ፡ ወበዓለ ፡ ተዝ ካር ፡ ኮከበ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ብሩህ ፡፡ ወጽዱል ፡ ዘመልዓ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዓለመ ።፡
1 In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. One kingdom and one power and one dominion and one will; one essence and a single eternity of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit: they are equal in eternity. The Father has no epoch nor a single hour without His Son and the Holy Spirit. Three names, one God: they are distinct in persons and dwelling without separation. And may the Lord be blessed and may His mention and His perfection be blessed, and may His clemency and His mercy be upon us, for ever and ever, amen. And now we say and narrate the beginning of the birth of Malke'a Krestos, pure and elect, sweet of name and good of fame, exalted in the struggle. And the feast of his commemoration, (like) the luminous morning star with its shining light, filled the whole world. The struggle of Malke'a Krestos
ወይእዜኒ ፡ ንንግር ፡ ወንዜኑ ፡ ፃማ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዘተጋደለ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ። ወዘከመሂ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ወመንከረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወአኅጉር ። በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ። ወዘንተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለመልክከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ነበበ ፡ ፳ ባህታዊ ፡ አምቅዱሳን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይርድኦ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘአለበዎ ፡ ለዕዝራ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወአስተየ ፡ ጽዋዓ ፡ እሳት ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ጐሥዓ ፡ ወነበበ ፡ ወመጻሕፍተ ፡ ፳ ወ፪ተ ፡ ወከ ማሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እነብብ ፡ ትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ በከመ ፡ አመረኒ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። አጽሕፍ ፡ ይደልወነኬ ። ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ንንግር ፡ ወንዜኑ ፡ የማ ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ እስመ ፡ ረባህ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ለነፍሳቲነ ፡= ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ፃማ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለዓቢይ ፡ ወከቡር ። ወትሩፍ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ወኅሩይ ፡ ርእ ሶሙ ፡ ለባህታውያን ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዘአስተርአየ ፡ ለነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፥ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ወፍቁር ፡ በኅበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወአመ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነደት ፡ ፍቅር ፡ አምላካዊት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ። ወቦአት ፡ ውስተ ፥ አባላቲሁ ፡ ፍርሃት ፡ መንፈሳዊት ። ዛቲ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እንተ ፡ አፍረየት ፡ ለእግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ፡ በዉጠወ፳ቿወ፻ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነነ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ ብዙኃ ፥ ንዋያቲሁ ፥ ወብዕሎ ። ወተድላሁ ፡ ዘየሐልፍ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ኮነ ፥ ይውዒ ፡ በላህበ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይገብር ፡ ትሩፋተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ በየህቅ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ከመ ፡ ያጥሪ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ መንፈሳዊተ ።
2 And now we say and narrate the toil of the struggle of Malke'a Krestos who fought in the land of his father and his mother, and how he also performed miracles and wonders in every land and city, for the name of our Lord Jesus Christ. And this book of the struggle of Malke'a Krestos, an anchorite of the saints thought of it, while the Holy Spirit helped him who made the prophet Ezra (2 Esd 9:26-28 etc.) intelligent and made him drink the cup of fire, and he then emitted and expressed the twenty-four books (2 Esd 14:38, 45); and in the same way, as He gave me the revelation, I will expose the virtues of Malke'a Krestos. I write as the Holy Spirit has indicated to me. Therefore it is good for us, O beloved, that we narrate and tell the tribulation of the struggle of the saints, because they are useful for our souls. And great indeed was the tribulation of the struggle of the great and honored and eminent, pure and elect head of the anchorites Malke'a Krestos, who appeared to us as he was before the Lord; elect and loved by all men for his goodness, in the time when divine love burned in his heart and spiritual fear entered into his limbs. This is what bore fruit for the Lord in the measure of thirty and sixty and a hundred (fold) (Mk 4:20). In fact he, by his own will, despised his many goods and his wealth and his comforts that pass, and instead he was inflamed with the ardor of the fire of the Holy Spirit. And he performed virtues every day with great diligence, to achieve the great spiritual works.

### Practice of works of mercy

3 And indeed he practiced abundantly every will of the Lord and drove away from his whole mind the enemy of the soul and of the body and always served the Lord, while he refrained from looking at the vain works of this world that passes quickly like a dream and fades like the darkness. And, instead of this, he desired spiritual works, remembering what the blessed Paul said: "Since you have died with Christ, seek (the things) above where Christ is, who sits at the right hand of God, not what is on the earth" (Col 3:1). In fact this saint, truly elect and pure, Malke'a Krestos, did not remember the inheritance of his father and his mother and their passing wealth, and carnal youth did not overcome him as (it overcomes) us who desire the world and the wealth on the earth, after us. Instead this elect Malke'a Krestos, young in the Holy Spirit, contemplated with his mind and made clear with his intellect and taught the hope of the martyrs, and the crowns that crowned them and the glories of which they were worthy, he threw behind him like trash. And thought nor error did not overcome him at all more than those; and he continued to crave the tribulation of Saint George and desired all his torments, and said: "I will die like the saint for the name of Christ and I will accept like him the piercings and the beatings with the hammer and to be sawn with the saw and to be wounded on the head with a heavy club and to be crushed (with) stones and to be scattered like dust and the wind in the desert, and (to suffer) amputations with the sword."
ወእንዘ ፥ ከመዝ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ወይውዒ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሳት ፡ ልቡናሁ ። ወይኩን ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ። ወከልዓቶ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አግዝአ-ት›ነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ በ ፪ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አም ላከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተሰምዖ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ ስዕል ፡ ዘዚአሃ ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ግበር ፡ ግብረ ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡ በኅቡዕ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ብሂላ ፡ ተሠወረት ፡ እምኔሁ ። ውእቱሰ ፡ አባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይቀንዕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወይፈቱ ፡ ያግህድ ፡ ሃይማኖቶ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ጐባዔ ። ወበቅድመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ወርኩስ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ይነብብ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ወይዔድሞ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ በኅቡዕ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትሩፋተ ። ወይገብር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሠናያተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጸጋ ፥ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኮነት ፡ ተዓቅቦ ። ወኮነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያጸንዖ ፡ በሉ ፡ ፍናሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይድኅፅ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓ ለም ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕደብሰ ፡ ፅኑዕ ። ወሶበ ፣ ጸውዖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትሩፋት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ ወይትሐሠይ ፡ ወአፍጠነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ገድል ፡ በጐጉዓ ። ወዓዲ ፡ እከሥት ፡ ትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ወንጹሕ ። እስመ ፡
The image of Mary speaks to Malke'a Krestos 4 And while he prayed thus, his mind burned like fire so that he might become a martyr for the right faith, because of the name of our Lord Jesus Christ. And our lady, the holy doubly-Virgin Mary Mother of God, prevented him from this, while she made him hear the voice from the mouth of her image: "As for you, perform the spiritual work in secret!". And having said thus, she disappeared from him. Therefore he, the elect Abba Malke'a Krestos, became zealous for the right faith, and desired to profess his faith before the assembly and before the heretical and unclean king, and furthermore he read the book of the struggle of Saint George. And it pleased before his eyes, and he performed in secret all the virtues and did every good for his Lord, because the grace of God protected him and the Lord fortified him in all his ways so that he would not err in perpetuity, so that he would not sin gravely. And when the Lord called him to the virtues, he became happy and content and began that spiritual combat with diligence. And again I will manifest the virtues of Malke'a Krestos, elect and pure, because his intellect makes me work with the grace of the Spirit


[p. 276] {#po.51.p276}
ሕሊናሁ ፡ ያጌብረኒ ፡ በጸጋሁ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ከመ ፡ አይድዕከሙ ፡ ሎ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ በጥያቄ ፡ ወይትፌሣህ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ያትገሀኒ ፥ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንብብ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ከብራቲሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘያብዕሉ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዮም ፡ በዓለ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ -፦=
Holy, so that I may narrate to you the whole story of the blessed one with accuracy, and my heart may rejoice. May the Holy Spirit make me attentive so that I may say in an excellent way the glories of this great luminous torch, the elect Malke'a Krestos, whose beloved ones celebrate today a great feast for him.

### The hagiographer asks for the Lord's help to write the struggle of the saint
ወይእዜኒ ፡ አንሰኬ ፡ እፈርህ ፡ ኦፍቁራንየ ። ከመ ፡ አውጥን ፡ ወአጽሕፍ ፡ መጽ ሐፈ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ኮከብ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወዓቢይ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ አአ ምር ፡ ድካመ ፡ ሕሊናየ ፡ ሕፀፀ ፡ አንአምለብ፡ንቢቦ ፡ ውዳሴያቲሁ ።፡ ወኢይትከ ዐለኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ አብጽሕ ፡ ፃማ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ዳዕሙ ፡ እሴፎ ፡ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈኑ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ጸደለ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ኃያል ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብርህ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ወየሀቦ ፡ ለልሳንየ ፡ ሕፁፀ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ከመ ፡ እንብብ ፡ ንስ ቲተ ፡ እምገድላቲሁ ፡ ጥዑማት ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ መፍቀርያነ ፡ ከርስቶሰ ፡፡ እስመ ፡ ዘከ ሮተ ፡ ገድሉሰ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ይትሌዓል ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕሊናየ ፡ ድኩም ፡ ወልሳንየ ፡ ጸያፍ ፡ ዘኢ
5 And now indeed, I too fear to begin, O my dear ones, and to write the book of the struggle of this luminous, exalted and great star, because I myself know the weakness of my intellect, the (my) smallness to understand his praises. And it is not possible for me to report the tribulation of the struggle of the elect Malke'a Krestos. However I trust that the Lord will send me the splendor of the light of this intrepid one, so that it may illuminate my heart and give to my lacking tongue the strength to express a little of his (spiritual) combats, agreeable to the people who love Christ. For indeed the memory of the struggle of the great Abba Malke'a Krestos rises above the intelligence of men and much more above my weak intelligence, and my stuttering tongue that cannot express his praise.
ይከል ፡ ነቢበ ፡ ውዳሴሁ ። ወባህቱ ፡ እስአል ፡ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀበኒ ፡ ልቡና ። ወእንግር ፡ ንስ ቲተ ፡ እምትሩፋተ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ወሃይማኖቶ ፡ ርት ዕተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በ፳፪ልብ ፡ ወተስፋሁ ፡ ፅንዕተ ። ወፍቅሮሂ ፡ ዘአ ልቦ ፡ ትምይንተ ። ወርኅራሁ ፡ ለሉ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ። ወይዋቴሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡፥ ሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዓቢያን ፡ ወንዑሳን ፡ ወጣዕመ ፡ ቃዖሉ ፡ ወመንኖቶ ፡ ከብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። ወአውሥኦቶ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ጐባዔ ፡ በተሀብሎ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ፍር ሃት ፡ ለግሙራ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ መዘምር ። ወአነብብ ፡ ስምዓከ ፡ በቅ ድመ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ወኢይትሐፈር ። ወእሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፣ ዘእከሥት ፡ ለከሙ ፡ ኅዳጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ። ወዘልፈ ፡ ይሄሊ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሰማያረዊ-ተ ። ወአኮ ፡ ምድራ ዊተ ። ወነበረ ፡፥ በተፅናሰ ፡ ወረኃብ ። ተዘኪሮ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየ ሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ(ዐን ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ይርኅቡ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይፀ ግቡ ። ወነበረ ፡ በፃማ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ እስመ ፡ በብ ዙኅ ፡ ፃማ ፡ ሀለወነ ፣ ንባዕ ፡ ለመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማይ ።፡ በረከቱ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስ ቶስ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፥ ገብሩ ፡ -<ገብረ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ወፍቅርቱ ፡ «ወለተ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡>
6 And I only ask the Lord that He may grant me the ability, and I may say a little of the virtues of the struggle of Abba Malke'a Krestos, elect, and his right faith for the Lord, with one heart, and his firm hope and his love without deceit and his meekness towards the whole human race, and his gentleness with all men, great and small, and the sweetness of his word and his contempt for the glory of this world. And his speaking manifestly before the assembly with boldness, without fear, always, as the psalmist David said: "I spoke of your testimonies before kings and was not ashamed" (Ps 119:46). And these are the things that I will manifest to you a little in this book. And incessantly he meditated on the heavenly work and not (the) earthly (one). And he lived in poverty and (in) hunger, remembering the word that our Lord Jesus Christ said: "Blessed are those who now hunger, for they will be satisfied" (Mt 5:6). And he lived with much tribulation, remembering his word that the apostle said: "It is through many tribulations that it is fitting for us to enter the kingdom of heaven" (Acts 14:22). May the blessing of Malke'a Krestos be with his servant [Gabra Sellase] and his beloved [Walatta Sellase] for ever and ever, amen. Parents and place of origin of Malke'a Krestos

7 In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God.

### It is therefore convenient that we tell you the origin of the progeny of the blessed Abba
ይደልወነኬ ፡ ንንግ ርከሙ ፡ ጥንተ ፡ ሙላዱ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ አባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ዘመልዓ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዓለመ ።፡፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዕንቄ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ ዘብዙኅ ፡ ጫጡ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ዘቀተሎ ፡ ለጐልያድ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ። ወአ ጋንንት ፡ እኩያን ። ስምዑኬ ፡ ኦሕዝበ ፡ ከርስቲያን ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ። ከመ ፡
Malke'a Krestos, sun of justice, whose light filled the whole world. This is the jewel of the pearl (Mt 13:45) that many bought; this is David who killed Goliath (2 Sam 21:19), that is the devil and the evil demons. progeny of the blessed and holy Malke'a Krestos, beloved of our Lord Jesus Christ and his apostle. And indeed there was a man, lover of the Lord, whose name was Ta'ema, and the name of his spouse was Awganya. And their region was Sarako under Yebaba and above Tul and the tabot was of Saint George. And they were both righteous and walked in all the law and the precepts and the judgments of the Lord and loved the Lord very much. And they were rich in gold and silver, and loved mercy towards the poor and the needy, because they remembered His word, as our Lord said in the gospel: "Blessed are the merciful, for they too will be shown mercy" (Mt 5:7). And every day they gave alms, but they were sad for the lack of children, and every day they prayed to the Lord that He might grant them an elect and good son in all his behavior. And they said to our Lord every day: "Grant us a wise son who gladdens his father and his mother." And afterwards, while they were praying to the Lord, the angel Saint Michael came to them and told them that (a son) would be born to them by the name of Malke'a Krestos, by the Holy Spirit, as Gabriel had told Zechariah that John would be born (Lk 1:13); and afterwards he disappeared from them. Birth of the saint
ወእምዝ ፥ ተሠወረ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ። ወሐወፀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአውጋንያ ፡ ሕሪት ። ወፀንሰት ፡ ወወለደት ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወኅሩየ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስ ቶስ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ወጸንዓ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወተምህረ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ብሉየ ፡ ወሐ ዲሰ ፡ ወወጠነ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ሠናያተ ። ወአትሐተ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለሉ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወን ዑስ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐዋርያት ። አትሕቱ ፡ ርእ ሰከሙ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በአማን ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡፥ እግዚእነ ፡ በወንጌል ። ብፁዓን ፡ ነዳያን ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወእምዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትሜሐር ፡ በምድረ ፡ በይራጊ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ አሀተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ እማኅደሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትዋነይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐፃናት ። ወተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘንእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወተናገረቶ ፡ በቃል ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትትዋነይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕፃ ናት ። አንተሰ ፡ ትትዋነይ ፡ ምስለ ፥ መላእከት ፡ መንፈሳውያን ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡
8 And the Lord visited the elect Awganya: and she conceived and bore a son beautiful and elect in the Holy Spirit. And this son, by the name of Malke'a Krestos, grew and was fortified in the Holy Spirit and learned the books of the Old and of the New (Testament) and began to do good works, and humbled himself to all, great and small, remembering the word that the blessed Peter, the head of the apostles, said: "Humble yourselves to all men in the Lord" (1 Pet 5:6). Truly what our Lord said in the gospel was fulfilled in him: "Blessed are the poor in spirit, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven" (Mt 5:3). And afterwards, while he was being instructed in the land of Bayragi, (where there is) the tabot of our lady Mary, one day he went out from his dwelling to play with the boys. And he returned back to our lady Mary, and she spoke to him with her voice, like humans, and said to him: "Why do you play with the boys? You indeed will play with the spiritual angels and with the blessed righteous and with the faithful elect and with the holy disciples."

ኀበ ፡ ዋልድባ ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ዳልሻሐ ፡ ወአብረንታ”ንት ፡፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ሥርዓታ ፡ ጥቀ ። ወኢይከሉ ፡ ፀዊሮታ ፡ ካልዓን ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፥ ውስቴታ ። ወተራከበ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ምስለ ፡ን መምሕር ፡ መንፈሳዊ ። ወአኮ ፡ ሥጋዊ ፡ ወፈጸመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትምሕርተ ፡ ዘመነኮ።ሳ›ት ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ፈጸመ ።
Mary and arrived at Waldebba and at Dal$aha and Abrantant. And the ordinance of that hermitage was very burdensome and other monks could not sustain it except those who were in it. And he found himself with a spiritual and not carnal master. And he completed all the teaching of the monks and perfected all his virtues.

### The doctrine of Leo and Nestorius on the nature of Christ
ወበከመዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለልዮን ፡ ርኩስ ፡ መፄ ዋነ5 ፡ ትምህርት ። ወደቂቁ ፡ ለንስጥሮስ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ መራራነ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወእለ ፡ አል ቦሙ ፡ ስም ፡ ወእሉ ፡ ተኩላተ ፡ ሮም ፡ እለ ፡ ይመስጡ ፡ አባግዓ ። ወይከፍሉ ፡ ለ፪ ባሕርየ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ወትስብዕት ። ወይቤሉ ፡ ባሕርየ ፡፥ መለኮት ፡ ፍሉጥ ፡ ለባሕ ቲቱ ፡ ወባሕርየ ፡ ትስብዕት ፡ ለባሕቲቱ ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ፳ ህላዌ ። አላ ፡ ፪ ቱ ፡ ጠባይ ዓት ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወኢተዋህደ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋ ። አላ ፡ ኃደረ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ።
10 And while he was living in this work, the children of the unclean Leo, deviators of the doctrine, came; and the children of the heretic Nestorius with bitter words and those who have no name and those wolves of Rome who snatch the sheep (Jn 10:12) and divide into two the nature of divinity and humanity. And they say: "The nature of divinity is separated, by itself, and the nature of humanity is by itself, and there is not a single being, but they are two natures. And the divinity is not united with the body, but dwells in it."

11 Let us return then to the beginning of the discourse of Malke'a Krestos. And such was the behavior of Malke'a Krestos; when he saw and read the struggle of (Saint) George, he wept much and made lamentation, and desired this hermitage and said in his heart: "I will die like the saint and blessed George and I will accept his sufferings with the axe and with the hatchet and with the cleaver and with the pike and with the club and with the saw with which he was sawn and with the fire with which they burned his bones, and (with) the sword with which the neck of the saint and blessed George was cut, and he took seven crowns. And our Lord Jesus Christ gave him the covenant and gave him the kingdom of glory that does not pass for ever and ever. And so I too will welcome those torments, for the name of our Lord Jesus Christ, and I will become a martyr for the right faith." And after this, he went out to fight and become a martyr for the faith and contended with (the adversaries) and defeated them. As for them, they said to him: "The divinity is not united with the body, but dwells in it, as the apostle said: 'And dwells in us' (2 Tim 1:14) - as (the right of faith) do not say -. What do you say?" The doctrine on the nature of Christ: answer of Malke'a Krestos

and was not united with the flesh'." And again he spoke to them; listen and understand: what he said to them is what the sacred books affirm. And the sacred books teach us that it did not become two natures, but the nature of the divinity. And Moses said in the law: "And he descended like dew on the wool" (Ps 72:6). David also said: "Blessed is he who comes in the name of the Lord" (Ps 118:26). Isaiah said: "Who is this glorious man who comes out of Edom?" (Is 63:1). Ezekiel also said: "Being he having been sent from heaven" (Ez 1:1). Jeremiah also said: "And I saw the son of the flesh". Solomon also said: "Wisdom built herself a house and erected seven columns" (Prov 9:1). Habakkuk also said: "I saw your work and was admired; I saw you between two animals" (Hab 3:2). And Saint Gabriel the archangel said: "The Holy Spirit will come upon you and the virtue of the Most High will overshadow you", as the apostle Luke wrote (1:35). And the blessed apostle Paul said: "And when the fullness of time (came), He sent His Son who was born of a woman" (Gal 4:4).

13 And furthermore he answered and said: "O hypocrites, examine and understand: and why do you separate (the divinity) from the humanity? He who indeed separates the divinity from the humanity, does not believe (in) three persons, rather he denies their union and the Trinity of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit. If you say that we teach, I will teach you what the doctors said." And the elect Malke'a Krestos said: "Listen and learn: the Father and the Son and the Holy Spirit are three, in the fire, and are inserted in the divinity. And the Three Hundred and Eighteen doctors say: 'I and my Father and the Holy Spirit, His light and His heat. I and my Father and the Holy Spirit: the fire, the flame and the ember'." And the Aragawi Manfasawi said: "Observe then what is said of the Father of all, how they compared Him to that pearl (Mt 13:45) that is sought. They compared the Son who is venerated by all, to the moisture of the water; and they compared the Holy Spirit who is glorified by all, to the flow of the water." And again, as its life and its word are not disjointed from the soul, so His Son and His Word and His Holy Spirit are not disjointed. The doctrine of the "Faith of the Fathers" on the nature of Christ 14 And Abba Cyriacus said: "Three names, one Lord." And John, patriarch of Alexandria, said: "The names are not identified among themselves: the (name of the) Father to be the name of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, and the name of the Son to be the name of the Father and of the Holy Spirit, and the name of the Spirit

ወወልድ ። ወይቤ ፡፥ መቃርዮስ ፡ ዘእስከንድርያ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ንስምዮ ። ለአብ ፡ አበ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ወላዲ ። ወለወልድኒ ፡ ወልደ ። በእንተ ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እምአብ ። ለመ ንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘወጽአ ፡ እምአብ ። ዓዲ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ሾ፻ <ወ ፲ቿ> ፡ ወዘይ ቤሉ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ከመቦ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አዕይንት ፡ ወአእዛን ። ወሉ ፡ ዘተብ ህለ ፡፣ እለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ነአምን ፡ ከመ ፡ አሙን ፡ ወጽድቅ ፡ ውእቱ ። ባሕቱ ፡ ኢይትረከብ ፡ ወኢይትሌለይ ። ወይቤ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ዘእስከንድ ርያ ፥ እስመ ፡ አብ ፡ በአካሉ ፡ ወበገጹ ፡ ወበመልከዑ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወልድኒ ፡ በአካሉ ፡ ወበገጹ ፡ ውእቱ ። መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፡ በአካሉ ፡ ወበገፁ ፡ ወመልከዑ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወኢይገምሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጓለ ፥ እመሕያው ። ወይቤ ፥ ጐርጎርዮስ ፡ ዘእን.ዚና›ዙ ፡ ወለለ ፳ አካላት ፡ ፍጹም ፡ በገጹ ፡ ወመልከዑ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወይቤ ፡ አግናጥዮስ ፡ ዘአንጾ ኪያ ፡ ኢይፈልስ ፡ አብ ፡ ለከዊነ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወልድኒ ፡ ለከዊነ ፡ አብ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፡ ለከዊነ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ።
Holy to be the name of the Father and of the Son." And Macarius of Alexandria said: "When we call the Father, (we say): 'Father', because He is the begetter; and the Son: 'Son', because He is begotten by the Father; the Holy Spirit, because He proceeds from the Father." The Three Hundred (and eighteen) also say what the scriptures say, as the Lord has eyes and ears, and we believe everything that is said of the remaining (senses) for the Lord is true and just; however He is not touched nor separated. And Athanasius of Alexandria said: "The Father is by His person and by His face and by His figure; and the Son is by His person and by His face; the Holy Spirit also is by His person and by His face and by His figure." And he does not understand Him like a man. And Gregory of Nazianzus said: "And each person is complete by his face and by his figure." And Ignatius of Antioch said: "The Father does not emigrate to become the Son and the Holy Spirit, nor the Son to become the Father and Holy Spirit, nor the Holy Spirit to become the Father and the Son."
ወይቤ ፡ ጐርጎርዮስ ፡ ዘእንዚናዙ ። ወለለ ፳ እምአካላት ፡ ኢይትዌለጡ ፡ እምህላ ዌሆሙ ፡ አብሂ ፡ ለከዊነ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወልድኒ ፡ ለከዊነ ፡ አብ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስሂ ፡ ለከዊነ ፡ አብ ፡ <ወ>ወልድ ። ወይቤ ፡ ሱንትዮስ ፡ ዘእስከንድርያ ። አብሂ ፡ አብ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወላዲ ፡ ኢተወ ላዲ ፡ ወልድሂ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ እምአብ ፡ አላ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ኢወላዲ ፡ አላ ፡ ተወላዲ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘሠረፀ ፡ እምአብ ። ኢወላዲ ፡ ወተወላዲ ። ወይቤ ፡ ጐርጎር-ዮ>ስ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንከራት ። አካሎሙሂ ፡ ለሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአስማቲሆሙ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይዴሐር ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ። አላ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ብሉያነ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወይቤሉ ፡ ፻፻ «ወ ፲ቿ> ፡ ኢሀሎ ፡ ዘመነ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወኢሀሎ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ መንፈስ ። አላ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ህልው ፡ በኩሉ ፡ አዝማን ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ዘእስክንድርያ ፡ ኢሐሎ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ። ወኢከመ ፡ ቅፅበተ ፡ ዓይን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወመንፈሱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። አላ ፡ ህልው ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምስሌ
15 And Gregory of Nazianzus said: "And each of the persons does not transform from their substance: the Father to become the Son and the Holy Spirit, nor the Son to become the Father and the Holy Spirit, nor the Holy Spirit to become the Father and the Son." And Sunteyos of Alexandria said: "The Father is Father, begetter, unbegotten at all times; the Son is begotten by the Father, but before he was not a begetter but begotten; and the Holy Spirit who proceeds from the Father is neither begetter nor begotten." And Gregory Thaumaturgus said: "Of the persons of the Holy Trinity and their names, none is the last among them, but they are the Ancient of Days." And the Three Hundred (and Eighteen) said: "There is no time without the Son and there is no Son without the Spirit, but the Holy Triad is in all times and in all days." And Basil of Alexandria said: "There was not an hour nor the twinkling of an eye without his Son and his Holy Spirit, but existence is with them at every moment."

### 16 And the Areopagite said: "The birth of the Son and the procession of the Holy Spirit
ሆሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወይቤ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ፋጎስ ፡ ልደተ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወፀዓተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እም አብ ፡ ይትነከር ፡ ወኢይትነገር ። ወይቤ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ዘቄሳርያ ። ንዕመን ፡ በሃይማ ኖት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትወረሌ>ድ ፡ መሰኮት ። በአብ ፡ እስመ ፡ በአብ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ወልድ ። መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ እምአብ ፡ በልደተ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወፀዓተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምአብ ፡ ይትወሀድ ፡ መለኮተ ፡ ሥላሴሁ ፡ ለአምላከነ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጠይቁነ ፡ ዋህድናሆ(ናንሙ ፡ ለ፻ ይቤሉ ፡ ፻፻ <ወ፲፳ ፡፥ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልድ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቫሉ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳ ዘሠረፀ ፡ እምአብ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ነአምን ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ። ወነአምን ፡ በአግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልድ ። ወነአምን ፡ በእግዚአብ
from the Father is wondrous and ineffable." And Basil of Caesarea said: "We believe by faith that the divinity is born in the Father, since in the Father the Son was born; the Holy Spirit also proceeded from the Father with the birth of the Son, and, (to)


[p. 286] {#po.51.p286}
ሔር ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይቤ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ዘእስከንድርያ ፡ ፳ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ አብ ፡ ዘኢተወልደ ፡ ወ፳ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋህድ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ። ወ ፳ውእቱ ፥፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ «ገብረ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡> ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜሩን> ።
the procession of the Holy Spirit from the Father, the divinity of the Trinity of our God is unique." And again, for us, they ensure the uniqueness of the three (persons); the Three Hundred (and Eighteen) say: "God the Father is over all and in all, and the Son is God over all and in all, and the Holy Spirit is God who proceeds from the Father." And we believe furthermore in God the Father and we believe in God the Son and we believe in God the Holy Spirit. And Athanasius of Alexandria said: "One is God the Father who was not begotten, and one is God the only-begotten Son, Jesus Christ, and one is God the Holy Spirit." May his prayer and his blessing be with his servant [Gabra Sellase], for
ወእምረዝ ፥» አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኅሩይ ፥፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ ወጠ ይቁ ፡ አብዳን ፡ ዘይቤሉ ፡ ሊቃውንት ። ወይቤ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ዘቂሳርያ ። ወዘይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስመ ፡ አርአያ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተአመነ ፡ በአ ካለ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ ለወልድ ። በከመ ፡ አካለ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ መለኮቶሙ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይቤ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ዘእስክንድርያ ። ወበሠለስቲሆሙ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ መለኮት ። ወይቤ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ዘአንጾኪያ ። መለኮትሰ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አካላት ፡ ዘው ስተ ፡ ገየት ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ወይቤ ፡ ከርስቶዶሉ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእስከንድርያ ፡ ናወ ግዝ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ መቅዶንዮስ ፡ ሙስንት ፡ ዘይከህድ ፡ መለኮተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይቤ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ። አም ላክ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወኢይትበሀሉ ፡ ሾ፻ አማልከት ። አላ ፡ ፳ አምላክ ። ወይቤ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ ወአርዮስ ፡፥ ፋጎስ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ዘያበርሁ ፡ በርሃናት ። ወዓዲ ፡ እብ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ቃሉኒ ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እምአብ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘእምብርሃን ።
ages of ages, amen! The doctrine of Malke'a Krestos, in accordance with the faith of the tradition. 17 And after this, the elect Malke'a Krestos answered and said: "Listen and examine, O fools, what the doctors say! And Basil of Caesarea spoke, and what he said is thus: '(The Son) is the image of the person of God; behold, he has believed in the person, (in) the existence of the divinity of the Son, as in the person, in the existence of the divinity of the Father and of the Holy Spirit.'" And John of Alexandria said: "And the divinity is in their Trinity." And Basil of Antioch said: "As for the divinity, the persons are the holy faces." And Christodulus, Patriarch of Alexandria, said: "We excommunicate the corrupt faith of Macedonius who denies the divinity of the Holy Spirit." And Athanasius said: "God is the Father, God is the Son, God is the Holy Spirit; and they are not called three gods, but one single God." And Dionysius the Areopagite said: "They are the lights that illuminate." And again: "The Father is light, also his Word, light that was born from the Father, is light from light. And likewise the Holy Spirit who proceeded from the Father is light from light."

18 And Athanasius said: "The Father is life-giving, the Son is life-giving, and the Holy Spirit is life-giving." And Sunteyos said: "Those who did not die are living." And Gregory said: "Source from source." And Cyriacus said: "The Father is fire, the Son is fire, the Holy Spirit is fire: unique is the fire, the life that (comes) from the highest heaven." And John of Antioch said: "And if we say three persons, the nature is complete in their persons." And Cyril said: "This

nature is not separated from the nature of the Father." And Severus said in the Mashafa Hedar: "Nature from nature." And Cyriacus said: "It is not fitting that nature be without person." And Sunteyos said: "If indeed there is no nature without person, neither is the person without nature, as is the assertion of the heretical, foolish men, blasphemers of the Word." And Dionysius said: "Nature is not found without person, neither above, in heaven, nor below, on earth." And James said: "He who has no helper has no separation between his mind and his word and his life. We believe that the Father is the mind and the Son is the word and the Holy Spirit is the life." And Hippolytus said: "The Son is the Word of the Father and of the Holy Spirit. And the Holy Spirit is the Spirit of the Father and of the Son." Testimonies of the Holy Scripture. 19 And the prophet David said: "By the word of the Lord the heavens were established and, by the breath of his mouth, all their virtues" (Ps 33:6). Indeed, we believe thus and we profess thus: the nature of the three persons and their Trinity, as (God) is one in divinity and three in persons. And in this fact, while (God) is three in persons and one in divinity, man disobeyed the commandment of his creator, and the Lord drove him out of the paradise of delight (Gen 3:23) and stripped him of the light with which he was clothed and clothed him in a tunic of skin in place of the light, and gave him the hope of being reborn from one of his daughters (Gen 3:15) who was our lady Mary. And when this hope arrived, the Word came, without separating from the bosom of his Father. And he dwelt in the womb of the Virgin and the Word became man from her. And the body is not his garment, but uniquely Son. And he said to teach this: "It was not the Father who dwelt in the womb to become incarnate, so that no one would say that the Father transferred himself to become (man), but it was the Son himself (who dwelt in the womb of the Virgin), by the will of his Father and with the good pleasure of the Holy Spirit, since the will of the Holy Triad is one." Testimonies of Gregory of Nyssa, John Chrysostom, Athanasius, Hippolytus, and others.

"No one participated in creating his body, but he himself created it." And John Chrysostom said: "For the Lord created for him a living sanctuary with the Holy Spirit." John of Alexandria said: "Then the flesh of our Lord Jesus Christ was elevated by the principle of the person, while it was united with the Word." And Zechariah said: "But he united himself to it in one divine essence, and became one person and one face and one essence by the unification, as light is united with the eye and hearing with the ear and splendor with the sun and heat with fire." And the learned Athanasius said: "How and with what likeness do we believe? (It is) the body that clothed the divinity. And he chose it and gave it life; it became an addition to the divinity, but the body found—he said—a great increase thanks to its participation and by its union with the Word; while (the body) was mortal and while it was carnal, (it became) spiritual. And while it was earthly, behold, it passed through the gate of the heaven of heavens." And Hippolytus said: "Who is the heavenly one who was found with the appearance of a man, for the body that he took from our lady Mary the Virgin? And he is powerful God, because the heavenly divinity has united with him." 21 And Gregory of Nazianzus said: "It is the Lord who became man, and it is the man who became God by the union of the divinity." And John Chrysostom said: "The created acquired glory, great glory, when it became the garment of the Creator." And he said: "He is equal to us: this is one only by the incarnation; and, of two essences, he became one only by the union." And furthermore, he created a new body from the lineage of David, as it was written: "And he made it one of the Holy Trinity, while it was not equaled with his being. And furthermore the Lord, with his own wealth, enriched our body with the conjunction that is inexpressible and inexplicable; and he made it holy, life-giving." And thus he answered them and said to them: "The books indeed teach us, O fools and blind of heart: what do you say and what do you answer?" And while he was saying this to them, their mouth (was silent), and they could not speak, and he overcame them. And the elect and pure Malke'a Krestos, having thus admonished them, returned to his seat. May his prayer and his blessing be with his beloved [Gabra Sellase], for ages of ages, amen!


[p. 292] {#po.51.p292}

### In the hermitage of Wasa with Abba Tasfa Hawaryat
ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በገዳሙ ፡ ዋሰ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወአሐ ዊሁ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ይሕንፁ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አደጋት ፡ ወአምዝ ፡ አም ከነዩ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፥ ኢይትፈለጡ ፡ እምአቡሆሙ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ተምዖሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወገሠፆሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ትርከቡ ፡ ማዕከለ ፡ ፻ አውግር ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ትኩል ፡ ወሕ ንፁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ከርስቲያነ ። ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ፈለገ ፥ ዘምሉዕ ፡ እስከ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዛራማ ፡፥ ወተመይጡ ፡ በፍርሃት ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡሆሙ ። ውእቱሰ ፡ ገሠፆሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ዘ(ኢን"እፎ ፡ ዘኢሠረርከሙ ፡ በከ ንፍ ፡ ዓድግኒ ፡ ይፃእ ፡ ለከ ፡ ከንፍ ። ወሠረረ ፡ በከንፍ ። ወቦ ፡ እምደቂቁ ፡ ዘሠ ረረ ፡ በከንፍ ። ወቦ ፡ ዘከፈሉ ፡ ባሕረ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ። ወመጽአ ፥ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ዘውአቱ ፡ ፈላሲ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ተሐንፁ ፡ በማኅ ደርየ ፡።<።፦
22 And while he was in his hermitage, Wasa, with his father Tasfa Hawaryat and his brother monks, he ordered his children to build the hermitage of Adagat. And after, many brought forward excuses not to separate from their father. As for him, he was very angry with them and rebuked them and said to them: "In the middle of three hills, you will find a column fixed: build a church upon it." And while they were walking on the journey, his children found a river in flood, up to its source, which is Zarama, and they returned with fear from their father. As for him, he rebuked them and said to them: "Why did you not fly with wings? Even to a donkey wings would sprout for you and it would fly with wings." And some of his children flew with wings, and some divided the river. And after, they did as he had commanded them. And a Jew came, that is, a foreigner, and said to them: "Why are you building on my property?"
ወዘንተ ፡ ርአዮሙ ፡ ለአኩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡሆሙ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ በፈቃደ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ይከውን ፡ ከርስቲያነ ። ወኮነ ፡ ከማሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቆመት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ከርስቲያን ። ወተሐንፀት ፡ እንዘ ፡ መላእከት ፡ ይረድእዎሙ ፡ ንቡረ ፡ እድ ፡ ዘይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወዳልሻሃ ፡ ፳ ውእቱ ፡ ሥርዓቱ ። እስመ ፡ ኢይስየም ፡ ፳ ሂ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ <በ>ፈቃደ ፡ መምህር ፡ ዘዳልሻሃ ። ወዘእአንበለ ፡ ያመከርዎ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ድልወ ። ወአመሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ድልወ ፡ ለሚመት ፡ የዓሥርዎ ፡ እደዊሁ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ ይሠይምዎ ። ወዛቲ ፡ ሥርዓት ፡ ለሉ ፡ ጻማቲሁ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ላዕሴሁ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ። እስፍንተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ተሐንፀ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ሆከ ። ወእንዘ ፡፥ ይጹሊ ፡ በአስተሐምሞ ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ድንግል ፡ በ ፪ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አም ላከ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ታቦትየ ፡ እስከ ፡ አርእየከ ፡ አነ ፡ መካናቲሆን ። ወአን ተኒ ፡ ሰዕል ፡ ወሑር ፡ ኀበ ፥ ኅበ ፡ ካልዕ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘቶ ። በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ <ገብረ ፥ ሥላሴ ፡= ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡
23 And, having seen this, they sent a message to their father; and he said to them: "By the will of the Lord, may he become a Christian." And it happened so. And after, that church arose and was built while the angels helped them. And the jurisdiction of the nebura ed of that hermitage and of Dal$aha is one, in fact no one was ordained except with the consent of the superior of Dal$aha and if in that hermitage they do not examine him as to whether he is worthy. And if then he is worthy of ordination, they bind his hands and after they ordain him. And this was the rule for all the labors of Malke'a Krestos. And one day the governor came to him, saying: "How many churches have you built?" And, having said this to him, he was troubled. And while he was praying devoutly, our pure lady, the doubly Virgin Mary, Mother of God, came to him and said to him: "Take my tabot, until I have shown you their places. And you pray and go to another (place)." And he did as she had ordered him. May his blessing be with his servant [Gabra Sellase], for ages of ages, amen! Reading of the life of Malke'a Krestos on the day of his feast.
አ-ረሜ>ን ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳ አምላክ ፡ ገድል ፡ ወትሩ ፋት ፡ ዘቅዱስ ፡ ወብፁዕ ፡ አባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ስም ፡ ወሠናየ ፡ ዜና ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ዘመልዓ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ለእግዚአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዘፈጸመ ፡ በድሮ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ወዓቀመ ፡ ሃይማኖቶ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡
24 In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. Spiritual combat and virtues of the blessed and holy Abba Malke'a Krestos, of sweet name and good reputation, sun of justice (Mal 4:2), whose light filled the world, the beloved of our Lord Jesus Christ who finished his course in good and

በዓቢይ ፡ ከብር ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ለሰኔ ፡ በሰላመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አመቱ ፡ «ወሰተ ፥ ሥላሴ ፡ ለዓለ-<መ> ፡ ዓለ-ም ፡ አሜ›ንጁ
kept his faith (2 Tim 4:7) and rested with great glory on the 10th of Sane in the peace of the Lord. May the prayer and the blessing of Malke'a Krestos be with his servant [Walatta Sellase] for ages of ages, amen!
ቀዳማዊ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ወይሄሉ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘኢይረከቦ ፡ ሙስና ፡ ያስተጋብዓነ ፡ ዮም ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኅሪት ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኅሩይ ፥ ዘኢያብደረ ፡ ሚመተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወከብሮ ፡ ዘ[ኢ)የኃልፍ ፡ ዳዕሙ ፡ ጠየቀ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወአሥሂፀ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቀዱስ ። ወአእመረ ፣ ተስፋ ፡ ዘተውኅቦሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ወለሰማዕት ፡ ወለጻድቃን ፡ ወከብረ ፡ ዘሃለዎሙ ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ጠየቀ ፡ ነደ ፡ ልቡ ፡ በፍቅር ፡ አምላካዊ ፡ አኦአኃውወየ ፡ ንዑ ፡ ንትጋባዕ ፡ ዮም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ በዓለ ፡ መርዓ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ነሐሊ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ። ወንበል ፡ በከመ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ዘኃረይኮ ፡ ወዘተወከፍኮ ፡ ወዘአ ኅደርኮ ፥ ውስተ ፡ አዕፃዲከ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ንበል ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘአንተ ፡ ገሠፅኮ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ በአማን ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ዳዊት ፥ን በላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ስብሐት ፡ ወከብር ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ለአምላከነ ፡ ለዘኃርዮ ፡ ወሠምሮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ አባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ትሩፋት ፡ ይኩን ፡ ለሊሁ ፡<፦>
25 He who is the first of all and will be before all, he who was not struck by corruption, has gathered us today, on this pleasant day, which is the day of the feast of Abba Malke'a Krestos, the elect, who did not choose the order of the world and its passing glory, but contemplated in his heart and made the fruit of the Holy Spirit sprout and knew the hope that was given to the apostles and the martyrs and the righteous, and the glory that they had. And his heart contemplated this (and) burned with divine love. O my brothers, behold, let us gather today in this feast of the wedding, while we sing to the Lord and say like the prophet David: "Blessed is he whom you have chosen and whom you have welcomed and whom you have made to dwell in your courts" (Ps 65:5); and again we say: "Blessed is the man whom you have instructed, O Lord" (Ps 94:12). Truly the prophecy of David was fulfilled in this saint. Praise and glory is due to our God, to him who chose him and was pleased with this holy and pure Abba Malke'a Krestos, so that he himself might be the prince of virtues. And there was in those days a heretical king...
ወኮነ ፡ በውእቱ ፥ መዋዕል ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ሥዩመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወነፍሐ ፡ ነፋሰ ፡ ዕልወት ። ወአኃዘ ፡ ይትቃረ ኖሙ ፡ ለከርስቲያን ፡ ወአንሥአ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ስደተ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ የኃዝን ፡ ፈድፋ(ፋንደ ፡ ወይበኪ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ትግራይ ፡ ወሐማሴን ፡ ወአምሐራ ፡ ወጎዣም ፡ ወሸዋ ፡ ወአስተር አየቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወነገረቶ ፡ ዘየሐውር ፡ ፍኖተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወቦዓ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሪቅ ፡ ወአንገርገረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወወረደ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ እመንበሩ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ አዕላፍ ፡ መላእከት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትኅ ዝን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢኮንከ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ ትከውን ፡ አበ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ መነኮ ሳት ፡ ወመነኮሳይያት ፡ ወገዳማት ፡ ይትሐነፁ ፡ በእዴከ ። ወብዙኃን ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ በቃልከ ፡ ኅበ ፡፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዓርገ ፥ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ሰማያተ ። በረከተ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ -ገብረ ፡ ሥላሴ :፥> ለዓለ-መ» ፡ ዓለም ፡ <አሜን> ።
26 And there was in those days a heretical king established with authority in the whole country, and he was very great, and a wind of heresy blew, and he began to oppose the Christians and stirred up a great persecution. And as for this elect Malke'a Krestos, having heard, he was greatly saddened and wept much; and after that, he went into the land of Tegray and Hamasen and Amhara and Gojjam and Shewa. And our lady Mary appeared to him and told him to set out on a journey. And therefore he arose and departed and entered into the land of Rige and rolled on the ground and wept much weeping because he had not become a martyr. And our Lord Jesus Christ descended from his exalted throne—and with him descended myriads of angels—and said to him: "Do not be saddened because you did not become a martyr; you indeed will become the father of many monks and nuns, and with your hands hermitages will be built, and by your word many peoples will be converted to the right faith." And after he had said this to him, he ascended from him into the heavens. May the blessing of his grace be with his servant [Gabra Sellase].


[p. 296] {#po.51.p296}

### The pious nun Walatta Seyon
«ወአስተርአዮ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእከት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወሑር ፡ ኀበ ፥ ወለተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ወርድኣ ፡ ወትረከብ ፡ ከብረ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ከብር ፡ ለይ እቲኒ ፡፥ ዜነዋ ፡ ከማሁ ፥ ዘይመጽእ ፥ ጊዜሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዜነዎ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፥፣ መላእከት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመርሖ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቀዱስ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ በነግህ ፡ ከመ ፥ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ርቅ ፡ ፍኖት ። ወጐድጐደ ፡ ጥ"ተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወርእየቶ ፡ ዓመታ ፡ ታበርህ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ወዜነወታ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ወትቤላ ፡ ቀይሕ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ወስሙ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወማዕከላይ ፥ ቆሙ ፡ ወልብሱ ፡ አነዳ ፡ እምሐቋሁ ፡ ኢየዓርግ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወኢአምአብራኪሁ ፡ ኢይወርድ ፡ ታሕተ ። ወይቀውም ፡ በአፍዓ ። ወትቤላ ፡ ለዓመታ ፡ ሑሪ ፡ ጸውዒዮ ። ወሖረት ፡ ወጸውዓቶ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወተአምኃ ፡ ወይእቲኒ ፡ አምነቶ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘፈነወከ ፥ ኅቤየ ። ወይቤላ ፥ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእከት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወዜነወኒ ፡ ዘአገብር ፡ አነ ፡ ወይእ ቲኒ ፡ አውሥአት ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ለተኒ ፡ ነገረኒ ፡ ከመዝ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤኪ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ተዜያነዉ ፡ ቨሎ ፡ እምጥንቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜቱ ።
27 [And Michael] the archangel [appeared to him] and said to him: "Arise and go to Walatta Seyon and help her and you will find much glory upon glory." He announced to him thus that the moment had come for her. And therefore he arose promptly, as the archangel Michael had told him, and went to her, while the Holy Spirit guided him. And he arrived at the time of the morning, like the angel of the Lord, since the journey was long, and knocked at the door of her house, and her servant Tabareh Seyon saw him, and our lady Walatta Seyon spoke to her and said to her: "His complexion is red and his name is Malke'a Krestos: he has a medium stature and a garment of skin that does not rise above his hips and does not descend below his knees and on the outside it is straight." And she said to her handmaid: "Go to call him." And she went and called him, and he entered to her and greeted her and she also greeted him, and after she said to him: "Who sent you to me?" And he said to her: "Michael the archangel came and told me what I (had to) do." And she answered and said to him: "To me also he spoke thus and said to me: 'At that hour he will come to you.'" And thus they told each other everything, from the beginning to the end: for ages of ages, amen! Dedication of the saint to works of good.
ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ንግባዕኬ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ወገድሱ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ ወከቡር ፡ ወጥዑመ ፡ ልሳን ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ክርስቶሰ ፡ ዘመልዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወዘልፈ ፡ ይሄሊ ፡ ሰማ ያተ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ወዓለምሰ ፡ ምድራዊት ፡ ኢይሄሊ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይልበሰ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለከርስቶስ ። ተዘኪሮ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መልእከቱ ። ወበከመ ፡ ለበስነ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለሰማያዊ ። ወካዕበ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀለዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ዘይማስን ፡ ይልበስ ፡ ዘ(ኢ)ንይማስን ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ መዋቲ ፡ ይልበስ ፡ ዘኢይ መውት ። ወ)ወኢይትሄየይ ፡ ተግሣፀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ኢታስተዓፅብ ፡ ተግሣፀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢትትገኃሥ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይዘልፈከ ። እስመ ፡ ለዘአፍቀረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቀ-ሥ>-ፍዎ ፡ ለሉ ፡ ውሉድ ፡ ዘይትፈቀድ ። ወኢየዓርፍ ፡ ሕቀ ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መልእከቱ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ዕብራውያን ፡ ናስተፋጥን ፡ ከመ ፡ ንባፅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንደቅ ፡ በከንቱ ፡ ከማሆሙ ፡ ለአሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፡ ዓለዉ ፡ ወያፃምዋ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፥ ወለአመ ፡ ይኤዝዞ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ይብል ፡ አሆ ፡ እመኒ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ንዑስ ። ወያቴሕሕት ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐዋርምት ፡ በውስተ ፡ መልእከቱ ፡ አትሕቱ ፡ ርእሰከሙ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወለኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ።
28 Let us return therefore to the virtues and to the struggle of the blessed and glorious, of sweet tongue, Malke'a Krestos. He was full of the Holy Spirit and always solicitous of the heavens of the creation, while he did not think for one hour of the earthly world, so that he might clothe himself in the likeness of Christ, remembering what the blessed apostle Paul said in his letter: "And as we have clothed ourselves in the likeness of the heavenly one" (1 Cor 15:49). And again: "He who was corrupt you clothe with that which does not corrupt, and that mortal clothed himself with immortality" (1 Cor 15:53-54). And he did not neglect the correction that came to him from the Lord, as the apostle said: "O my son, do not consider the chastisement of the Lord annoying and do not withdraw from him when he corrects you, since the Lord (corrects) those whom he loves and strikes all those whom he wants as sons" (Heb 12:5-6). And he did not stop a little from doing good, remembering what the blessed (apostle) said in his letter to the Hebrews: "Let us hasten to enter into his rest so that we do not fall in vain" (Heb 4:11), like those who disobeyed and wearied his soul. And to whoever commanded him, whether great or small, he said yes to all, and bowed his head to all, remembering what the blessed Peter, head of the apostles, said in his letter: "Lower your head to all the creatures of the Lord and to all the creatures of men for the Lord" (1 Pet 2:13).


[p. 298] {#po.51.p298}

### He did good to please his creator
ወይገብር ፡ ሠናያተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አሥምሮ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ ወኢይብል ፡ ደከምኩ ። ወገደፋ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕደው ። ወኢይብል ፡ ምንተ ፡ እበልዕ ፡ ወምንተ ፡ እለ ብስ ። ወጐየ ፡ እምሐውዘ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወኢየኃሥሥ ፡ ተድላሃ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በአማን ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚአነ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይበቀዖ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ረብሐ ፡ ወነፍሶ ፡ ኃጐለ ፡ ወያፈቅርዎ ፡ ኩሎረሙ> ፡ አሐው ፡ እስመ ፡ ያኃ ሥሮሙ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ወበምግባሩ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወይጹሊ ፡ በኅቡዕ ። ወለለዕለቱ ፡ ይሰግድ ፡ ፻፻ ስግደታተ ። ወይገብር ፡ አስተብርኮ ፡ ወአድንኖ ፡ በንዑሳት ፡ ወበዓቢይት ፡ በዓ ላት ። ወለኩሉ ፡ ትሩፋቲሁ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ጐልቀኑ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ መስፈርት ። ወሶበሂ ፡ ይሴፅር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢይበጽሕ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሆባ"ይ ፡ ወሐለስትዮ ፡ ወግሄያት ፡ ወአዕዋፍ ፡ ወይፀውር ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ዘኢይከልዎ ፡ ፪ ዕደው ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘርእዮ ፡ ያነከር ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ወኅሩይ ፡ እምሠናይ ፡ ተዓዝዞቱ ።
29 And he did good to please his creator and did not say: "I am tired." And he threw his life away like trash and did not say: "What will I eat and what will I wear?" (Mt 6:31). And he fled from the pleasures of the world and did not seek the delights of the world; truly what our Lord said was fulfilled in him: "What does it profit a man if he gains the whole world and loses his soul?" (Mt 16:26). And all the brothers loved him, since he submitted himself to them with his word and with his good work and prayed in secret, and every day he performed three hundred prostrations; and he made genuflections and prostrations both in the small and in the great feasts; and there is no number and there is no measure to all his virtues. And when he cut wood, (he went) where man did not reach, but only the eagle and the monkey and the marmots and the birds, and he carried as much as six men could not; and whoever saw him remained amazed at the work of Malke'a Krestos, pure and elect for his beautiful obedience. Malke'a Krestos priest and abbot.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝ-ን›ቱ ፡ አዘዘቶ ፡ እሙ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ቅስና ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ዋልድባ ፡ ወተመጠወ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕጋጋቲሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ። ወነሥአ ፡ ቅስና ። ወሜምዎ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ። ወኃደግዋ ፡ ለይ እቲ ፡ ገዳም ። ወሐነፃ ፡ ለገዳመ ፡ አደጋት ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃው ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተርአ የቶ ፥ እግዝእትነ ፡ «ማርያም ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ። ወኃደጋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ። ንንግርኬ ፡ ዕበያቲሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ። እስመ ፡ ኢንትነብሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ትጉሃን ። ወአኮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐከይያን ። ወአኃዊሁ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ሐሊበ ፡፥ ድንግል ፡ ወደመ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ወቶማስ ። ወካልዓን ፡ አኃው ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ተመሰሉ ፡ በሕ ይወቶሙ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ መላእከት ። ወአሉ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ ሐዋር ያት ፡ ዘሐቅለ ፡ ዋሊ ፡ ወውእቱሂ ፡ ዘአስተናገረ ፡ ጽዋዓ ፡ ወዕብነ ። ወይእቲ ፡ ሐቅል ፥ ገዳሙ ፡ ለአባ ፡ «ሳሙኤል := መነኮስ ፡ ኃያል ፡ ወመስተጋድል ፡ ውእቱ ። ወለይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ባረካ ፥ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ። አመ ፡ ቦኡ ፡፣ ኅቤሃ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወተከዕወ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ጉልቅቆ ። ወለይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ታፈቅራ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ረማርያም ፡ እስመ ፡ ተቀደ
30 And after this, his (spiritual) mother Walatta Seyon ordered him to receive the priesthood. And therefore he arose and went to Waldebba and received all the rules of that hermitage and received the priesthood and they named him abbot. And they left that hermitage, and he built the hermitage of Adagat with the brothers. And after, our lady (Mary) appeared to him and said to him: "Arise!" And he left that hermitage. We will say therefore the greatnesses of that hermitage, since in it you have remained vigilant and so that you may not be idle. And the brothers of Malke'a Krestos, Haliba Dengel and Dama Krestos and Tomas and other brothers who were (there), with their life, imitators of the angels; and all of them were children of Abba Tasfa Hawaryat of the hermitage of Wali. And he then was the one who made the chalice and the stone speak. And that desert was the hermitage of the father (Samuel), strong monk and combatant; and our Lord Jesus Christ blessed that hermitage, when the holy fathers entered into it and in it the blood of countless saints was shed. And our lady (Mary) loved that hermitage, since it had been sanctified with her name and had been blessed with the hand of the God of Samuel and with his prayer, amen! Construction of the hermitage of Adagat and death of Walatta Seyon.
ሰት ፡ በስማ ። ወተባረከት ፡ በእደ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ለሳሙኤል ። ወበጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሐነየ ፡ ለአደጋት ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃው ። ወወጠነ ፡ ይኅንፅ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ከርስቲያ-ና»ት ፡ ለባሕቲቱ ፡ መስለ ፡ ደቂቁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተርእየቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡
31 And after this he built Adagat with the brothers, and he began to build the churches for himself alone, with his children. And after, our lady appeared to him

(Mary) and said to him: "Arise and depart!" And when he heard this, he arose promptly and went into the land of Maèéa. And when he arrived, he found himself with the inhabitants of that region, and therefore, the children of Takla Haymanot who were in the city of Segeg gave him the seat and the cross, and he remained with them for a little time. And then our lady, the holy doubly Virgin Mary Mother of God, appeared to him, and said to him: "Arise from this place and go to Walatta Seyon; in fact the time of her rest has arrived." And that (Walatta) Seyon indeed had sustained the spiritual combat in secret and had made three garments, and she wore an (iron hairshirt) and thus wearied her body, and no man knew her virtues until the day of her death. And even our father did not see her face until he made her funeral shroud; and thus our father did as she had told him. And after this, he went into the land of Shewa and found his mother Walatta Seyon and comforted her and she explained to him everything that was in her heart; then he consoled her in accordance with what she had narrated to him before. And she died with honor and glory and entered into the kingdom of heaven. He therefore made her commemoration and built a church and placed the tabot in it and constituted many monks and nuns. May his blessing be with his servant [Gabra Sellase], for ages of ages, amen! The Virgin Mary orders him to go to the district of Sayent.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አዘዘቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሁር ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሳይ ንት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፥ ወሖረ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሳይንት ፡ ወደከመ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ወበከ ይዎ ፡ ደቂቁ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አባ ፡ ተፃዓን ፡ ዲበ ፡ በቅል ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አነ ፡ አድግ ፡ እፀዓንኑ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓድግ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ ተሠዓን ፡ አሣዕነ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አነ ፡ መሬት ፡ እፎኑ ፡ እጫዓን ፡ አሣእነ ። ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ወፋንጭ ፡ ወእግረ ፡ ጥቀ ። ወበጽሑ ፡ የዳጀ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወኅበ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ። ወአስተ ርአረዮዬቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወንጽሕት ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ። ወአመ ረቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕንፅ ፡ ግሽቅ ። ወተባረከት ፡ ይእቲ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ዋልድባ ፡ ወማዕከለ ፡ በሻሎ ፡ ወምንጭቅ ፡ ፪ቱ ፡ አፍላግ ፡ ሀለወ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ አግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፥ ወነገረቶ ፥ ኩሎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይገብር ።
32 And after this our lady Mary commanded him to go to the district of Sayent. And therefore he arose and went to the district of Sayent, and he was tired from the journey, and his children grieved for him and said to him: "Abba, mount the mule." And he answered and said to them: "I am a donkey: shall I mount on (another) donkey?" And they said to him again: "Put on the sandals." And he said to them: "I am dust (Gen 3:19): shall I put on sandals?" And while they were in such things, they arrived in the land of Wafaneî, completely on foot. And they arrived at Yadaga, at the tabot of Abraham and at the tabot of Mary the Virgin. And our lady, the Virgin and pure Mary, Mother of God, appeared to him. And she indicated to him to build Gesag and that (monastery) was blessed like Waldebba, and it is in the middle of the two rivers Baselo and Menteq. And after, our lady Mary appeared to him and told him everything, how he would do it.


[p. 302] {#po.51.p302}

### The spiritual daughter Sabla Wangel
ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዳውንት ፡ ሀገራ ፡ ለሰብለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ወለቱ ፡ ወሐነፀ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕረት ። ማርያም ፡ ዘይብልዋ ፡ መካካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ሐነፀ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ዘትሰመይ ፡ ዓፀፍት ። ወይ እቲ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ዘእ.ግዝአ:ትነ ፡ ማርያም ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ እግዝ”እትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወነገረቶ ፡ ዘይገብር ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡፥፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ፍልድፍ ድላ ፡ እንተ ፥ ይእቲ ፡ ሰለልኩላ ። ወአንበረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ። ወበአንባሂ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ በ ፪ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ። ወፈለጠ ፥ ሥርዓተ ፡ በበአቶሙ ፡ ኢይየዑ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እምአ ንባ ፡፥ ወመነኮሳይያት ፡ ኢይባዓ ፡ ውስቴቱ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እግዝ እትነ ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ ወነገረቶ ፡ ዘይገብሮ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ በቀናዕይ መሸ ፡ በወንዛጌ ፡ ወበየጥራ ፡ ጣጅ ፡ ውሀ ፡፥ ወበእሉ ፡ ገዳማት ፡ አብከገኃ ፡ ጸልዮ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወበእንተ ፡ ሱሉ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ እጓለ ፡፥ እመሕያው ። ወበእ ንተ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ዘይቤሎሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ በሌሊተ ፡ ዓርብ ፡ ጸልዩ ፡ ከመ ፡
33 And after he arose and went to the city of Dawent, the city of Sabla Wangel his (spiritual) daughter, and he built there the tabot of Kidana Mehrat Maryam, which they called Makaka. And he built also the tabot of Kidana Mehrat which is called 'Adafet. And that tabot is of our lady Mary. And after, our lady Mary appeared to him and told him what he would do. And after this he arose and went to Feldefdela, which is Salalkulla, and placed there the tabot of Michael the archangel, and in Anba the tabot of our lady, the doubly pure Virgin, Mary, Mother of God. And he distinguished the arrangement for their entry: the monks were not to go out of Anba and the nuns were not to enter there. And after this our lady Mary appeared to him and told him what he would do. And after he arose and prayed in Qana'eymasa, in Wanzage and in Yatra Tag Weha, and in those hermitages he increased his prayer before the Lord, and for all the creatures of the children of man and for his disciples who were with him and for himself; he prayed, remembering the word that our Lord said to his disciples on the night of Friday: "Pray, so that you do not enter into temptation, since the spirit is ready, and the flesh is weak" (Mt 26:41).
ኢትባኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንሱት ። እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ወሥጋ ፡ ይደከም ። ወበከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ለጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ አስ ተበቀዓከ ፡ ሉ ፡ን ቀዳሜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ትግበር ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ወስኢለ ፡ ወተጋንዮ ። በእንተዝ ፡ ጸልዩ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ወመኳንንት ፡ ከመ ፡ በኅዱዕ ፡ ወበፅምው ፡ ይኩን ፡ ንብረትነ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወንጽሕ ። ዝኬ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወኅሩይ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መድኃኒነ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይኅ-የ-›ዉ ፡ ወያእምርዎ ፡ ለጽድቅ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወእፈቅድ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይጸልዩ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ገጸ ፡ መካን ። ወያንሥኡ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ በንጽሕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ነጐርጓር ፡ ወኑፋቄ ።
34 And as the blessed apostle Paul said to his disciple Timothy: "I beseech you first of all to make prayers and orations and intercessions" (1 Tim 2:1). And for this pray "for all men and for all kings and governors so that our stay may take place in quiet and in tranquility, in all justice and purity" (1 Tim 2:2). This therefore was good and elect in the sight of the Lord, our Redeemer, since he wanted all men to live and know justice. And he said again: "And I desire that all men pray in every kind of place and raise their hands with purity, without murmuring and hypocrisy."

### Instructions to his disciples

36 And thus, my children, do you also, do not love yourselves, but your neighbor; and 'what you would want men to do to you, so also you do to them' (Mt 7:12), as our Lord said in the gospel; and this is the commandment of the Lord. We believe in his Son Jesus Christ and let us love one another; he gave us his commandment, my brothers, the whole spirit: do not despise it, but let us scrutinize the spirit if it is from the Lord, 'since many false prophets have come into the world' (1 Jn 4:1), who do not believe in Jesus Christ. As our Lord said in the gospel: 'Be careful and watch out for false prophets who come with sheep's clothing, but who inside are wolves and rapacious. And by their fruits you will recognize them' (Mt 7:15); and listen to me, your father, my children. 'And every spirit that believes that Jesus Christ has come into the world in the flesh of man, is (from) God. And every spirit that does not believe that Jesus Christ has come in the flesh of man, is not from God; and this is a false messiah' (1 Jn 4:2-3), as the blessed apostle said." Love of God and of neighbor.

37 And he said again to his children: "Love the Lord with all your heart and with all your soul and with all your strength (Deut 6:5), and thus love one another. And love the foreigner and the pilgrim, since the blessed apostle John shows us and teaches us, as for this the Lord was united with us, saying: 'And whoever believes that Jesus is the Son of God, God remains with him and he remains with God. And we also know and believe that the love of God is with us. And God is love; whoever remains in his love, remains with the Lord and the Lord with him' (1 Jn 4:15-16). And for this his love is fulfilled with us, so that we find his face with him on the day of judgment. Since it is useless that we are in this world (if) in our love there is no fear, but on the contrary, perfect love casts out fear. Since fear (supposes) this chastisement, and whoever fears is not perfect in love. And we also love one another and let us love the Lord, since he first loved us. And if someone says that he loves the Lord and hates his neighbor, he is a liar.

38 Since whoever loves his neighbor whom he sees, loves the Lord whom he does not see, whoever does not love his neighbor whom he sees, how will he be able to love the Lord whom he does not see? And this is the commandment that we have taken from him, so that we love the Lord: and whoever loves the Lord, loves his neighbor (1 Jn 4:17-21). And whoever believes that Jesus is the Messiah, is born of God; and whoever loves the begetter, loves him who was begotten by him. And from this we know that we love

ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ፡ ወተወልደ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ አፍቀርናሁ ፡ ለአግዚአብ ሔር ። ወገበርነ ፡ ትእዛዞ ። እስመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፍቅሩ ፡ ወትእዛዙ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ናፍቅር ፡ ቢጸነ ፡ ወንዕቀብ ፡ ትእዛዘ ። ወትእዛዙኒ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ከቡደ ። እስመ ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይመውዖ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወዛቲ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሙዓቱ ፡ ለዓ ለም ፡ እንተ ፡ ሞዖ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ሃይማኖትከሙ ። ወከመዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አጠየ ቀነ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተፋቅሮ ፡ ይጌይስ ፡ አምኩሉ ፡ ምግባር ። አንትሙኒ ፡ ፅቀቡ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወአጽንዑ ፡ ልበከሙ ። ወኢትትገሀሠ ፥ ኢለየማን ፡ ወኢለፀጋም ። እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፡ ወዘይጌይስ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ፍቅር ። ወበዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፥ አጠይቆሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተፋቅሮ ፡ የዓቢ ፡ እምኩሉ ። በረከቱ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ የሃሉ ፣ ምስለ ፥ ገብሩ ። «ገብረ ፡ ሥላሴ> ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወእምዝ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ በራዕይ ፡ ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ወት ቤሎ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወሑር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አባ ፡ አሮን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አባ ፡ አሮን ። ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወእንዘ ፥ ይጹሊ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ በዊዖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጾላዕት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እስከ ፡ ማዕዜኑ ፡ ታመነድበነ ፡ እስፍንተ ፡ ገዳማተ ፡ ኃደጉ ፡ ለከ ፡ ኢየ አከለከኑ ፡ ዘኃደጉ ፡ ለከ ፡ መካነ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ እሰብር ፡ ፪ተ ፡ እደዊከ ። ወ ፪እገ ሪከ ፡፥ ወእቀጠቅጥ ፡ በእዕባን ፡ ርእሰከ ። ወአመትር ፡ ልሳነከ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይረድአከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኢያአመርከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረዳእየ ፥ ወኃይልየ ። አዕቡይ ፡ ወርኩስ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ አማዕተበ ፡ ገጾ ፡ በት እ”ምርተ ፡ መስቀል ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ። ወተ ከሥተ ፡ 1ሎንሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርድአ ። ወአስተኃፈሮ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወኢገብዓ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያመከሮ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ በሐዲስ ፡ አምሃ ። ወበካልዕ ፡ ገዳም ። ወዓዲ ፡ እስተርአየቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፥ ማርያም ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወሑር ፡ ምድረ ፡ ጋይ ንት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ጋይንት ። ወሐነፀ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ፈየሳ ። ወበውስቴታ ፡ አንበረ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ወድንግል ፡ በ ፪ ማር ያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አስተርእአየቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ንጽ ሕት ፡ ድንግል ፡ በ፪ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡
God the Father and that God (the Son) was begotten: if we love God and observe his commandment, since this is the love and the commandment of the Lord that we love our neighbor and observe the commandment. And his commandment is not burdensome, since whoever is born of God has conquered the world; and this is the victory that has conquered the world, your faith' (1 Jn 5:1-4). And while he was saying thus, the apostle indicated to us how love is better than all works. You also keep this and strengthen your heart and do not deviate neither to the right nor to the left, since there is no thing greater and better than this love." And with this discourse he indicated to his children that love is greater than everything. May the blessing of Malke'a Krestos be with his servant [Gabra Sellase], for ages of ages, amen! Another appearance of the Virgin Mary. 39 And after, our lady Mary appeared to him in a vision and spoke to him and said to Malke'a Krestos: "Arise and go to the hermitage of Abba Aron." And after he arose and went to the hermitage of Abba Aron, and prayed for him; and while he was praying in that (hermitage), having entered into the rock, Satan came to him and said to him: "Until when will you persecute us? How many monasteries have I left you? Is it not enough for you that they leave you a place? And now I will break your two hands and your two feet and I will trample your head with stones and I will cut your tongue. And now, who is it that will help you?" And our father said to him: "Do you not know that Christ is my help and my strength, O insolent and unclean one?" And having said this, he signed his face with the sign of the cross. And after, our Lord descended from heaven to our father and manifested himself to him to help him, and shamed Satan (who) did not return to him to tempt him. And after he prayed with a new greeting and in another hermitage. And again our lady Mary appeared to him and said to him: "Arise and go to the land of Gayent." And after he arose and went to the land of Gayent, and built in it the hermitage of Fayasa, and placed there the tabot of our lady, the pure and doubly Virgin Mary, Mother of God. And after this our lady, the pure, doubly Virgin Mary, Mother of God, appeared to him, and said to him: "Depart from this hermitage and go to the land of Guna." In the land of Guna.
ገዳም ፡ ወሑር ፡ ምድረ ፡ ጉና ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቃላ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ድንግል ፡ በ ፪ ማርያም ፡ ወላ ዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ደንገፀ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወአፍጠነ ፡ ሐዊረ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ምድረ ፥ ጉና ። ወዓርገ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእከት ፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡
40 And when he heard the voice of our pure lady, the doubly Virgin Mary Mother of God, he was amazed and rejoiced and arose and walked in haste and arrived in the land of Guna. And he went up to Dabra Mika'el the archangel and Gabre'al the archangel, who ask for mercy for (his) children, and who supplicate

መላእከት ፡ እለ ፥ ይስእሉ ፡ ምሕተ"ተ፤ ፡ ለውሉደ ። ወይሰግዱ ፡ ወያስተበርኩ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ እግዚኦሙ ፡ ዔር ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ሥርየት ፡ ለኃጥዓን ። ወይእቲ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ጉና ፡ እንተ ፡ ትውኅዝ ፡ ሐሊበ ፡ ወመዓረ ፡ ሕንብ ርታ ፡ ወአከሊላ ፡ ለኢትዮጵያ ። ወተቀደሰት ፡ በነሶሳወ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሠርፀ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወዘልፈ ፡ ይሔውፅዋ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ መላእከት ፡ ጥዕምት ፡ በቃላ ፡ ወሠናይት ፡ በምግባራ ። ወቃላ ፡ ይኒ ፡ እምወይን ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ አባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወከቡር ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ወነጸረ ፡ መዓዝኒሃ ፡ የማና ፡ ወፀጋማ ።
and prostrate themselves before God, their good and just Lord, until the redemption of sinners comes. And that city of Guna, which flows with milk and honey, is the navel and the crown of Ethiopia, and it is sanctified by the walking of the feet of Abba Sarsa Mika'el, and the archangels always visit it: it is sweet for its voice and good for its works. And its voice is better than wine; and that great and honored Abba Malke'a Krestos went up to its summit and explored its corners, to the right and to the left.
ወጸለየ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጠ ። ወነ ጸረ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዓይነ ፡ ጉና ፡ ምዝብርት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወበ ጽሐ ፡ ገዳማተ ፡ ላሊበላ ። ወቦአ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ወተመጠወ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ሞርባነ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሙጀ ። ወሐነፀ ፡ በህየ ፡ መቅደሰ ፥፣ ማርያም ፡ እግዝእትነ ። ወሖረ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ወገራ ። ወቦአ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አደ ጋት ። ወነሥአ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ዘሠወረ ፡ ቅድመ ። ወእአን”በለ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተፈልጠ ፡ አምአኃ ዊሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ እስከ ፡ አርአየከ ፡ መካናቲሆሙ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ አበርገሌ ፡፥ ጥቃሃ ፡ ለፈለገ ፡ ተከዜ ። ወበህየ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወእንስሳ ። በእንተ ፡ ዝናማት ፡ ወማ ያተ ፡ አፍላግ ። በእንተ ፡ ፍሬያተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ለነኒ ፡ የሐውጸነ ፡ በረድኤቱ ፡ ወበም ሕረቱ ፡ ለአምላከ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፦። ወአስተርአየቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ወድንግል ፡ በ ፪ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ መካን ። ወነገረቶ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይገብር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘቶ ። ወሖረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐንፅ ፡ ዘወድቀ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ከርስቲያን ። ወያነሥእ ፥ ዘወድቀ ፡ መዝበራ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ። አሐንፅ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ደብተራ ፡ ዘዳዊት ። ወአንሥአ ፡ ዘወድቀ ፡ መዝበራ ። ወቦ ፡ ዘተነ በየ ፥ ላዕለ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ብዙኃ ፥ ትንቢተ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ተ-ን-ቤን ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ድንግል ፡ <በ> ፪ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ። ወአመረቶ ፡ ዘይገብር ።
41 And he prayed to the Lord his God for the (city), and afterwards he turned back and looked as far as the destroyed fountain of Guna. And after that he arose and went and reached the hermitages of Lalibala and entered the house of Mary our Lady, and he received the Eucharist in it; and afterwards he went down into the land of Muga and built there the sanctuary of Mary, our Lady. And he departed from there and reached Wagara and entered Dabra Adagat. And he took the tabot that he had hidden and which he had previously put away, at the time when he had separated from his brothers, as our Lady Mary had said to him: "Take the tabot until I have shown you their places." And he arose and went to Abargalle near the Takazze river and there he prayed many days for men and for animals, for the rains and the waters of the rivers; for the fruits of the earth. Provide also for us with the help and with the mercy of the God of Malke'a Krestos, our father, for ever and ever! Third apparition of the Virgin Mary 42 And our Lady, the pure and doubly Virgin Mary, Mother of God, appeared to him and said to him: "Arise from this place." And she told him how he would do it; and afterwards he arose as she had commanded him. And he went to rebuild a church that had collapsed and he made the ruins that had fallen rise again, as the prophet Isaiah said: "Build," he said, "the tabernacle of David" (Am 9:11). And he rebuilt the ruins that had collapsed. And someone prophesied many prophecies about his children. And after this he reached Temben and afterwards our Lady, the pure doubly Virgin Mary, Mother of God, appeared to him and indicated to him what he would do.

### In the district of Gar'alta
ወዘንተ ፡፣ ሰሚዖ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወሖረ ፡ ገርዓልታ ፡ ቦዘተበዩ ፡ ትን ቢተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ደቂቁ ። ወቦዘሐነፁ ፡ ገዳመ ። ለአሐቲ ፡ ይብልዋ ፡ አባዕሮ ። ወካልዕታ ፡ አድሂዛ ፡ ወአሐቲ ፥ መጋብ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ንጽ ሕት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ማርያም ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ። ወነገረቶ ፡ ዘይገብር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቃላ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ወርዕ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ላዕለ ፥ አውገር ። ወቦአ ፡ እንትጮ ፡ ትኩዝ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ደብር ፡ ወሐነፀ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ፪ አውራኃ ። ወካዕበ ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ እግዝ-እ> ትነ ፥ ንጽሕት ፥ ድንግል ፡ በ ፪ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ወአመረቶ ፡ ዘይገብር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ወግረ ፡ አንባሰነይት ። ወተፈልጡ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወዖዱ ፣ ሀገረ ፡ ይምነ ፡ ወፅግመ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተጋብዑ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እግረ ፡ ፀድፍ ።
43 And having heard this, he arose promptly and went into Gar'alta. There are many prophecies that were prophesied for his children; and those who built the hermitage: one they called Aba'ro and another Adhiza and one Magab. And after this our Lady, the pure Virgin Mother of God, Mary, appeared to him and said to him: "Arise," and explained to him what he would do. And when he heard her voice, he arose and went to the Ware' river and climbed the hills. And he entered Enteèto Tekuz, the holy monastery, and built Dabra Kidana Mehrat and stayed two months. And our Lady, the pure doubly Virgin Mary Mother of God, appeared to him again and indicated to him what he would do. And afterwards he returned to the hill of Ambasanayt and his children separated from him and went around the city, to the right and to the left; and afterwards they gathered together at the foot of a precipice. The monastery of Dabra Maryam
ወነበሩ ፡ ፪ አውራኃ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ፍኖቶሙ ። ወቦኡ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ማርያም ፡ በጎሩቱ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወበጸሎቱ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ መ"ልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዘመልዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ጸፍጸፍ ፡ ጸፍጸፍሂ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ያንጸፈጽፍ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወቅዱሳን ፡ አፅናቀኑ ፡ ያንኩ=<ረ-ኩሩ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ። እስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ሣረራ ፡ ቀዳሙ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ሐነጻ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወፈጸማ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ተቅ ዋመ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ ዘኩለንታሃ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወያከንት ፡ ፲ መሐትዊሃ ፡ ወጂ መሣውር ፡ ዘዲቤሃ ፡ ወ ፪አዕፁቀ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ በየማና ፡ ወአሀቲ ፡ በፀጋማ ። ወ፪አጥባት ፡ ይሠወጡ ፡ በመኃትዊሃ ። ወ ፪ አዕፁቀ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ ጠላት ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ካህናቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። በከመ ፡ አቅደመነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ራዕየ ፡ ወር እየ ፡ አምሳሊሃ ፡ ለሐደስ ፡ መርዓት ፡ ፳ እምነቢያት ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ በራከያ ፡ ወትሴሰይ ፡ ወትረ ፡ መዓዛሁ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማይ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ አጥ ፍዖታ ፡ ለፍቅር ። ወአፍላግኒ ፡ ኢያንቀልቅልዋ ። ወየኦዱ ፡ አድባር ፡ ወአውግር ፡ ለደ ብረ ፡ ማርያም ፡ በየማና ፡ ወበቅድሜሃ ፡ የዖዱ ፡ ወበፀጋማ ፡ <ለ>ማርያም ፡ ወላ ዲተ ፡ አምላከ ። ወአመረቶ ፡ መንበረ ። ወዘን-ተ>- ፡፥ ሰሚዖ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ገርዓልታ ፡ በዘተነበየ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ደቂቁ ።
44 And they stayed two months. And after this they arose and went on their way, and entered Dabra Maryam by the virtue of the Holy Spirit and by the prayer of the blessed and holy Malke'a Krestos, upon whom is the fullness of the Holy Spirit. And the name of that Dabra Maryam is Safsaf. Safsaf then means: the blessing drips upon her and the holy gems descend upon her. In fact, "first the Father founded her, and then the Son built her and the Holy Spirit finished her." And that support of the candelabrum is all gold and hyacinth: seven are its lamps and seven are the snuffers upon it and two olive branches upon it, one at its right and one at its left, and two little channels pour into its lamps. And the two olive branches (which drip) drops of molten (gold) are the priests of the Lord (Zc 4:2-3, 12-14). As one of the prophets, Zechariah son of Berechiah, anticipated for us that vision and saw the figure of a new bride (Zc 2:14; 8:12). And the perfume of the Holy Spirit is always maintained; abundant water cannot quench love and rivers will not sweep it away (Ct 8:7). And they went around the mountains and the hills of Dabra Maryam, to its right and in front of it they went around, and to the left of Mary, the Mother of God; and she indicated the seat to him. And having heard this, he arose promptly and went to Gar'alta, prophesying a long prophecy about his children.

### Branches of Dabra Maryam

45 And some built a hermitage which they called Aba'ero and another Ad Hiza and another Magab. And after this, our Lady the pure, the Virgin Mother of God Mary, appeared to him and said to him: "Arise," and told him what he would do. And when he heard her voice, he arose and went to the Ware' river. And he climbed the hills and entered Ἐπίθξξο and looked at Tekuz, where a cross of light was planted that had descended from heaven so that it might be the salvation for the men of Enteéto. And he built a church there and placed in it the tabot of Kidana Mehrat that he had brought with him from the hermitage of Wali and scattered its dust (there) and said: "This is my resting place for ever: I will dwell here, for I have chosen it" (Ps 132:14). And he stayed two (months) blessing and sanctifying the men of Tekuz. And our Lady the pure, the doubly Virgin Mary, Mother of God, appeared to him again and indicated to him what he would do. And afterwards he returned to the hill of Ambasanayt, and his children separated from him and went around the city, to the right and to the left. And afterwards they gathered in Sadef and stayed two months.

46 And after this, they arose and went on their way and entered Dabra Maryam by the virtue of the Holy Spirit and by the prayer of the blessed and holy Malke'a Krestos, upon whom is the fullness of the Holy Spirit, and its name is Dabra Maryam Safsaf; Safsaf then means: the blessing drips upon her and the holy gems descend upon her. In fact, first the Father founded her, and then the Son built her and the Holy Spirit finished her. And the support of the candelabrum is all gold and hyacinth: seven are its lamps and seven are the snuffers upon it, and two olive branches upon it, one to the right and one to the left, and two little channels pour into its lamps. And the two olive branches (which drip) drops of molten (gold), are the priests of the Lord (Zc 4:2-3, 12-14). As one of the prophets, Zechariah son of Berechiah, anticipated this vision and saw the figure of a new bride (Zc 2:14; 8:12). And the perfume of the (Holy) Spirit is always maintained; abundant water (cannot) quench love and rivers will not sweep it away (Ct 8:7). And they went around the mountains and the hills of Dabra Maryam, to its right and to its left, in front of her and behind her the torrid region; and to the east of it there was a high mountain and on its top two stone horns, and the other mountain had a cleft. And that Safsaf looks to the north, like the mountains of Zion, those on the north side. And the other (mountain) then which is found (there is) of the kings, from Kafal to Gegawa, from the last to Take'ewa, from Qadiba to 'Addi Qawa.

### Virtues and teachings of Malke'a Krestos

47 And we will narrate to you the completion of the struggle of this truly pure reverend, Malke'a Krestos. And it happened, after this elect had endured all the sufferings to obtain the promise with patience, as our Lord said in the gospel: "And with your patience you will possess your souls" (Lk 21:19). And as the blessed apostle Paul said: "Perseverance is the proof and with the proof hope is found, and hope does not disappoint" (Rm 5:5). And having thought this, he loved patience and every suffering. And thus he ordered and said to them: "Watch therefore, my children, and do not sleep and rejoice in your suffering and in your patience. And remember what the blessed apostle Paul said in his first letter: 'And they only considered this suffering of this world, as if it were not the quantity of the price of that honor and of that glory which is for ever' (Rm 8:18)."

48 The prophet David also taught this, saying: "If the Lord does not build the house, the (builders) labor in vain, and if the Lord does not guard the city, those who guard (it) watch in vain" (Ps 126:1). And thus he taught us how there is no quantity of price for that suffering of this passing world. As for you, as living stones, you have been built, as a house of the Holy Spirit (1Pt 2:5), so that you may be children of (him who is the) Good (Mt 19:17) and a dwelling of the Holy Spirit. Let your conduct be thus, so that you have not been freed in vain, which is useless; 'let your loins be girded and your lamps lit for the Lord' (Lk 12:35). Know and understand what our Lord said to his disciples: "The measure of the disciple, after he has become (a disciple), is the measure of his head, like his master" (Mt 10:25). And, remembering this, imitate me and do not walk in the counsel of the wicked (Ps 1:1). And do not be submissive to the Lord with a double heart, since the blessed apostle James said: "The double-hearted is unstable in all his ways" (Jm 1:8). Sanctification of the Sabbath

49 And after this he said to them: "Know and consider that the Sabbath day is the greatest of all days, since the Lord sanctified the seventh day, and in it he rested from all his work, having completed the creation that is in heaven and that is on earth (Ex 20:11). And again the Lord said to Moses: 'Six days you shall do your work, and on the seventh day let there be rest for your wife and for your cattle, and your ox and all your house shall rest'" (Ex 20:9-10). And he said again to his children: "Do not forget what our Lord said with the word of the prophet Isaiah: 'Blessed is he who honors the Sabbaths (Is 56:2) and makes them joy: they will magnify him and glorify him, they will purify him from his many sins'." Sabbath (Ex 31:13-14): you will inherit the mountain of my sanctuary, and my elect their fathers, the mountain of my sanctuary in the house, and the prayer. And thus the scriptures of the holy prophets taught us: that the Sabbath is greater than all days, that Sunday is great and honored. As for our Lord, he said in the gospel: 'Doing good is better on the Sabbath than any sacrifice' (Lk 13:14-15; 14:3). The prophet Moses also said in the Orit 'Do not raise your hands and your feet on the Sabbath to do any of your will (Ex 20:8) and do not let foul words come out of your mouth; put your trust in him and he will act for you (Ps 36:5). He will compensate you and will feed you the inheritance of Jacob your father.' And our Lord also teaches us that the day of Sunday is honored, since on it he rose at midnight and taught his disciples (Jn 20:19)." Sanctification of Sunday

50 And the blessed Malke'a Krestos spoke thus again and said to his children: "Observe the word of the apostles and of the doctors and which was written in their Synod. And thus the apostles said: 'For works they will serve their masters five days, while on the day of Sabbath and on the day of Sunday they will dedicate themselves to the church so that they may learn the ministries of the Lord. In fact, on the Sabbath day our Lord rested from all his work, since he had finished the creation of the creatures. And on the Sunday day he rose from the dead; and on all Sabbath days, except only the day of Easter, you will receive the Eucharist at the third hour. And on every Sunday day you will meet among yourselves in church and you will rejoice. And behold: at the beginning of the fast, it was said that they should not fast on Sunday and on Sabbath, except the Sabbath on which our Lord Jesus Christ was laid in the tomb.' And again the apostles said in the Didascalia: 'And it is necessary that you, my brothers who have been purchased with his precious blood which is the blood of Jesus Christ, celebrate the feast of Easter with all diligence and great solicitude after having eaten the unleavened bread; and after this feast, which is the memorial of the passion of our only-begotten Savior, twice in a year: once because he died for us and once for his resurrection.'" Festive rest

24 of the new moon of the month, in the other week in which they celebrate Easter. And do not celebrate the feast of the resurrection of our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ except on the day of Sunday alone. And thus the doctors of Laodicea affirmed about the Sabbath and said: "It is not fitting that Christians should not make work burdensome on the Sabbath day, like the Jews, but that they should work on it like Christians. And if any believers are found to remain, (and to practice) the works of the Jews, they will be cast out from the sight of the presence of Christ." And the doctors of Nicaea said: "Do not observe the Sabbath like the Jews." And again the apostles said in the Synod: "And let it not be on the Sabbath day and on the Sunday day." And the glorious feasts have no prostration, since those days are of joy. And for this reason we must refrain from working on the Sabbath day and on the Sunday day and on the feasts. And this chapter was not written but for the Sabbath. And again the sent apostles said: "And those who establish the deliberations, those invested with power who administer justice, punishment and judgment, will remove the Jews from all the faithful on the Sabbath day. And no one on that day will seek goods from his neighbor nor will solicit the faithful to claim a credit or something similar to this. On Sunday all must go to church.
