52 And on that (day) all people will go to church, and all the faithful, one by one, will come there, with purity and with humility, without fear of the governor or the creditor or the judge and the like. And if one of the tax collectors dares to take the tribute from those who go to church, he will return double." And the apostles say again in the Didascalia: "They gathered every day in church, and much more on the Sabbath day and on the day of the resurrection which is Sunday. And behold, we saw how the people did not leave the meeting on the day of its feasts: on that day they were all together. And thus those said that the congregation of the Jews was worthless. And the Jews ceased their work which was established for them: (it was) ceased when they gathered after six days. And they gathered on the seventh day in their temple and ceased their work which was established for them: (it was) ceased when they gathered. And, if they were truly vigilant for their meeting, it was advantageous for them. What then will you say to the Lord, you who have left the church?"


[p. 320] {#po.51.p320}

53 And the apostles say in the 23rd chapter: "And it is not fitting that you discuss a thing in which there is no advantage, or do not do a work that is not good. And much more on Sunday, in which it is fitting that you enjoy spiritual joy. As the prophet David said: 'Serve the Lord in fear and exult to him with trembling' (Ps 2:11). And the commentator said 'Understand the word that David said: This is the day that the Lord has made: let us exult and rejoice in it' (Ps 117:24), since it is the day of Sunday. And the apostles said again in the Synod: 'And the servants then (will work) five days, while on the Sabbath day and on the Sunday day they will be assiduous to the worship of the church that the envoys of the Lord taught. Since on the Sabbath day, when he had finished the creations, he rested from all his work. And on the Sunday day he rose from the dead, and for this he sanctified and glorified and magnified, more than any day, the Sabbath day, and likewise he glorified the Sunday day and magnified it more than any day.'" Instructions of Malke'a Krestos regarding the sanctification of feasts
ወከመዝ ፡ አጠየቆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ አለተ ፡ እሁድ ። ወይ ቤሎሙ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ወከቡር ፡ አባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ዘያበርህ ፡ ስኑ ፥ በውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ። ኦደቂቅየ ፡ አንትሙኒ ፡ ፅቀቡ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ እለተ ፡ እሁድ ። እስመ ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሰንበተ ፡ ከርስቲያን ፡ ወረሰያ ፡ ዕርፍተ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ። ወኃጥዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ይትኩነን ፡ በእሳት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። አመሰ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ዘያከብሩ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሰንበት ። ወእ ለተ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ እለተ ፡ እድ ፡ ይወጽኡ ፡ አምኩነኔ ፡ ዘእሳት ፡ ዘኢይጠ ፍዕ ። ወዕፄሁ ፡ ዘኢይነውም ፡ በቀዳሚ ፡ ሠርከ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወያዐርፉ ፡ እምኩነኔ ፡ ዘኢይጠፍዕ ፡ ወአምዕፄሁ ፡ ዘኢይነውም ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወበእለተ ፡ እድ ፡ ወይነብሩ ፡ በገነተ ፡ ትፍ[ፍንሥሕት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይወጽኡ ፡ እምገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ። ወይትዌከፍዎሙ ። አጋንንት ፡ እኩያን ። ወይወስድዎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያቴከልዎሙ ፡ ወያጸድፍዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘኢይ-ጠ-ፍዕ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ኃጥዓን ፡ ከርስቲያን ፡ እለ ፡ ያከብሩ ፡ ሰንበታተ ፡ በዕረፍት ፡ ወበስታይ ፡ እስከ ፡ የኃልፍ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕረያ›ው ፡ በደመና ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኃይል ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ በየማኑ ፡ ለእ ግዚእነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወኢየሐውሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሳት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። አላ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳ-ተ- ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለደ ቂቁ ፡ ተዘከሩ ፡ ዘተገብረ ፡ በአለተ ፡ እድ ።
54 And thus, the blessed and glorious abba Malke'a Krestos - sun of justice whose beauty shines in heaven and on earth - taught them about the Sabbath and about the Sunday day: "O my children, you too be observant about the Sabbath day and the Sunday day, since the Sabbath of the Christians is great, and he made it rest for sinners. And the sinners who committed sins will be condemned in the eternal fire. But if they have honored the Sabbath day and the day of the resurrection, which is the Sunday day, they will come out of the condemnation of the unquenchable fire - whose worm does not sleep (Mk 9:43) -, on the first evening of the Sabbath, and they will be dismissed from the unquenchable condemnation and from its worm that does not sleep, on the Sabbath day and on the Sunday day, and they will dwell in the paradise of delight. And afterwards they will come out of the paradise of delight and the evil demons will welcome them and will cast them out while they will precipitate them and throw them into the unquenchable fire. And thus the Christian sinners who honor the Sabbaths will remain, resting and drinking, until this world passes and until our Lord, the Son of man, comes on the cloud of heaven with power (Mt 24:30). And on that day they will stand at the right of our Lord with the just and will not go into the eternal fire, but into the fire of eternal life." And after this he said to his children: "Remember what was done on the Sunday day."

አውሥአ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወከቡር ፡ አባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ በእለተ ፡ እድ ፡ ወረደ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ። እንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ ጽዮን ። ወካዕበ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ ወለብዉ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አቡቀለምሲስ ። ወይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ በአሳት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተመሠጥኩ ፡ ወሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ አምድኅሬሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ቀርን ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ጸሐፍ ፡ እስኩ ፡ ዘትሬኢ ፡ ወትሰምዕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽ ሐፍ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወለብዉ ፡ ዘተጽሕፈ ፡ በአብረከሲስ ። ወተናገረ ፡ ሉቃስ ፡ ወይቤ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ኃለፉ ፡ በባሕር ፡ እለ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ አምነ ፡ ጳፉ ፡ ሀገር ። ወበጽሑ ፡ ጴር ጌን ፥ ዘጵንፍልያ ። ወኃደጎሙ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወተሠውጠ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ኃለፉ ፡ እምነ ፡ ጴርጌን ። ወበጽሑ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ዘጵስዲ(1ል)ንያ ። ወቦዑ ፡ ምኩራበ ፡ በእለተ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወነበሩ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አንበቡ ፡ ኦሪተ ፡ ወነቢያተ ፡ ለአኩ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ካህናት ። ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ አኃዊነ ፡ ለእመቦ ፡ አምው ስቴትከሙ ፡ ዘይከል ፡፥ ሠናየ ፡ ነገረ ፥ አይድዕዎሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ጳው ሎስ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ያርምሙ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወእለ ፡ ትፈርህዎ ፥ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከሙ ። ወነገሮሙ ፡ ሴሰዮሙ ፡ መና ፡ ጓ ዓመተ ። ወከመ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ መሳፍንተ ፡ ፪፻ወዛ ፡ ወአንገሠ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሳኦልሃ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቂስ ። ወዳዊትሃ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እሴይ ። ወእምዘርአ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ ኢየሱስሃ ። በከመ ፡ አሰፈዎሙ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በአለተ ፡ እሁድ ። እመሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አእምሩ ፡ ወጠይቁ ፡
The Holy Spirit descended on the apostles on the Sunday day 55 The great and honored abba Malke'a Krestos spoke again and said to them: "For Sunday the Holy Spirit descended on the apostles, while they were in the upper room of Zion (Ac 2:4, 6). And listen again and know what the great apostle John Abugalamsis said. And he said thus: "The Spirit came upon him in fire. And immediately I was rapt and I heard a loud voice speaking from behind him, like the sound of a horn. And he said to me: Write therefore promptly in the book what you have seen and heard' (Rv 1:10-11). And understand what is written in the Acts of the Apostles and Luke affirmed and said: 'And afterwards, those of Paul went by sea: from the city of Paphos they reached Perga of Pamphylia, and John left them and returned to Jerusalem. And they departed from Perga and reached Antioch of Pisidia; and they entered the temple on the Sabbath day and sat down. And after having read the law and the prophets, the heads of the priests sent to them and said to them: You are our brothers: if there is among you who can (say) a good word, speak to the people. And Paul arose and ordered silence and said to them: Listen, men of Israel and those who fear the Lord your God' (Ac 13:13-16). And he narrated to them about their nourishment of manna for forty years and how they had Judges for four hundred and fifty (years) and he made Saul son of Kish and David son of Jesse reign for them; and from his lineage the Lord brought out the salvation of Israel, Jesus, as he had made them hope; and everything that happened on the Sunday day (Ac 13:17-30). And if all this happened, know and let it be known to you, that the Sunday day is great and honored more than any day. Jesus Christ was conceived on the Sunday day
ከመ ፡ እለተ ፡ እድ ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ወከብርት ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ዕለት በዕለተ ፡ እኑድ ፡ ተፀንሰ ። ወበእለተ ፡ እድ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሱቱ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ ስመዓ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ዘአፈቅር ፡ ወሎቱ ፡ ሰምዕዎ ። ወበእለተ ፥፡ እኑድ ፡ ኮነ ፥ ተንሣኤሁ ፡ ለአግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኃኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡፣ ከርስቶስ ፡ ወበእለተ ፡ እጐድ ፡ ይከውን ፥ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፥ ወአሜሃ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ። ወያስተርኢ ፡ በግርማ ፡ መንግሥት ። ለኩሉ ፡ እምሥ(ቅንራቅ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምዕራብ ፡ እምሰሜን ፡ እስከ ፡ ደቡብ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ዘማቴዎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ መበረቅ ፡ ዘይወጽእ ፡ አምጽባሕ ፡ ወያ ስተርኢ ፥ እስከ ፡ ዓረብ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአሜሃ ፡ ይትነፋህ ፡ ቀርን ፡ በቀዳሚ ፡ ይትነፋሕ ፡ ቀርን ፡ ይትጋባፅ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በዐለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘተዘርወ ። ወበደግም ፡ ንፍሐተ ፡ ቀርን ፡ ይሰፍያ ፡ አዕፅምት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋ ። ወይከውን ፡ በድን ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አንሰሕስሖ ። ወበሣልስ ፡ ንፍሐተ ፡ ቀርን ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡ ሙታን ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅጽበተ ፡ ዓይን ።
56 He was conceived on the Sunday day and on the Sunday day, the Father was a witness for his Son, saying: 'This is my beloved Son: listen to him' (Lk 9:35). And on the Sunday day the resurrection of our Lord and our redeemer Jesus Christ happened, and on the Sunday day the coming of the Lord Jesus Christ will happen and then the Son will sit on the throne of his glory. And he will appear to all in the majesty of royalty, from east to west, from north to south, as he says in the gospel of Matthew: "As the lightning that comes out of the east and appears to the west, so will be the coming of the Son of God' (Mt 24:27). And then the horn will be sounded; at the first sound of the horn, every possessor of flesh, who had been dispersed, will gather. And at the second sound of the horn, the bones will reunite with the flesh, and it will be a complete corpse, without movement; and at the third sound of the horn the dead will rise in the twinkling of an eye.

57 As the blessed apostle Paul said: 'But we will all be transformed in an instant, like the twinkling of an eye. And the horn will sound and the dead will rise, without suffering harm; and we too will transform, since, being made of that which corrupts, we will clothe ourselves with incorruptibility, and he who is mortal will clothe himself with immortality; and when the mortal has clothed himself with that which is immortal' (1Cor 15:51-54), then our works will appear and will manifest themselves, and everything we have done will be read, as the apostle said, since everything is manifest and open before him. Then the sinners will be separated from the midst of the just, and our Lord will place the sinners at his left and the just at his right. As for the just, they will go into eternal life, while the sinners will go into eternal condemnation. And all this will happen on the Sabbath day." Sunday is the greatest of all days 58 He spoke again and said to his children: "Know and understand and let it be known to you with all this similitude, that the Sabbath day is holy, since the Sabbath day and the Sunday day for the just is happiness, and death for the sinners." And the elect abba Malke'a Krestos said to them: "You too honor the day of my Sabbath and the day of Sunday. And teach all men that the Sabbath day is holy and the Sunday day is the greatest of all days. Thus do and thus say on the Sabbath day and on the Sunday day. As the apostle James, brother of our Lord Jesus Christ, said: 'Thus speak and thus act as those who must be judged according to the law of the free, since in the final judgment there will be no mercy for those who have not used mercy here. And, he who has used mercy, will glory on the day of judgment' (Jm 2:13)." Here was finished, in the peace of the Lord, the discourse of the day of the first Sabbath and of the final Sunday. (With) the strength of the help of Malke'a Krestos, the elect, you honor and observe the Sabbath day. And thus may our father protect his servant Gabra Sellase and his bride Walatta Sellase and his father [empty space] and his mother [empty space] for ever and ever, amen. The Virgin Mary and the Covenant of Mercy

59 Let us return therefore to the completion of the struggle of Malke'a Krestos, elect by the will of the Lord. And while he was in Dabra Maryam, which they call Tekuz, which he named Gannata while he was performing miracles and wonders without number, our Lady came, the holy and pure doubly Virgin Mary Mother of God, to him and said to him three times: "This is my place of rest for ever." And thus, while she was with her beloved Son Jesus

Christ, she told him how she had given him the Covenant of Mercy for those who would invoke her name and would make her commemoration: "And thus, as the hermitage is my rest for ever, also for you it will be your rest for ever." And having heard this, he wept a bitter weeping, saying: "How will this hermitage be my rest, while it is the hermitage of Wali that our Lord blessed and that you love much more than all the hermitages?". And the blessed Mary answered him and said to him: "It is my Son who blessed it before, but now I have come with my Son, so that I and my Son may bless this hermitage for you, Dabra Maryam Tekuz which is called Gannata." And the great and honored Malke'a Krestos answered and said to her: "Bless for me, my lady, as you have said, this Gadama Tekuz, with its appurtenances and its branches: the name of the first is Ad Alala and of the second Ad Qayahat and of the third Makaram and of the fourth Ad TeSum, with the place of Kesad."
ወአምድኅረዝ ፡ ባረካት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መካናተ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ፻ ጊዜ ፡ እንዘ ፥ ትብል ፡ ትኩን ፡ ለከ ፡ ቡርከተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ትኩዝ ፡ ወሉ ፡ ውስቴታ ። አምሳለ ፡ ጌቴሴማን ፡ መቃብርየ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ዘሀደረ ፡ ቃል ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ወደብረ ፡ ታቦር ፡ ዘከሠተ ፡ ሎሙ ፥ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ምሥጢሮ ፡ ወረለ›ዘተቀብረ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ወለዘጸለየ ፡ ወለዘተማኅፀነ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ መሐርኩ ፡ ለኪ ። ወለሉ ፡ ዘጸውዓ ፡ ስምየ ፡ ወስመከ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ እምህሮ ፡ ወአሰምዖ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ወአድኅኖ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ምንዳቤሁ ። ወአበውዖ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡፥ ሰማያት ፡ ለዘወሀበ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ወመባዓ ፡ ወወይነ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ እምህሮ ፡ ወአሬስዮ ፡ ዕሩየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱሳንየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወረዱ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ማዕከለ ፡ ዛቲ ፥ ገዳመ ፡ ትኩዝ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወዖድዋ ፡ ይምነ ፡ ወፅግመ ፡ በቅድሜሃ ፡ ወበድኅ ሬሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ መካነ ፡ ትኩዝ ፡ እምጽንፍ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጽንፍ ፡ እምእግር ፡ እስከ ፡ ርእስ ፡ እምቄላቲሃ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ላዕላይ ፡ ጸድፍ ፡ መላእከት ፡ አዕላፍ ፡ እኀዛነ ፡ ሰይፍ ። አምሳለ ፡ ደመና ፡ ይኬልልዋ ፡ በከንፍ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አውሥአቶ ፡ እግዝእ ትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ወድንግል ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መል ከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ የዓጥኑ ፡ ለከ ፡ መቃብሪከ ፡ መላእ"ከት ፡ ወቅዱሳት ፡ ዘዋሊ ፡ ዘለሰዓቱ ። እለ ፡ ይፄዓኑ ፡ 1እንበደመና ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ነጌያት ፡ ወአናብስት ፡ ወአናምርት ፡ እስከ ፡ የሐልቅ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘመን ። ወእስከ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ደይ ፥ መሬተ ፡ ዘሀቅለ ፡ ዋሊ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ለደብረ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕ ረት ፡ ትኩዝ ፡ እለ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ለከ ፡ አርድዕት ፡ ዘቅዱሳን ። እስከ ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡ ለከ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍትከ ፡ ፍጹመ ።
The Virgin blesses the hermitages of the saint 60 And then she blessed these places for him three times with the sign of the cross, saying: "Be blessed for you this Gadama Tekuz and everything that is in it, like Gethsemane, my tomb, and like Mount Sinai, upon which the word dwelt, and like the Mount of Olives and Mount Tabor, in which he manifested his mystery to the apostles, and I will have mercy for you on whoever will be buried in it and on whoever will pray and on whoever will entrust himself to it. And together I will have mercy on all those who will invoke my name and your name and, having heard his prayer, I will free him from every tribulation of his. And I will make enter into the kingdom of heaven whoever will give incense and offerings and wine for this hermitage, together I will have mercy and I will make him equal to my saints." And afterwards the angels descended into the midst of this holy Gadama Tekuz, and went around, to the right and to the left, in front and behind this place of Tekuz, from end to end, from the feet to the head, from the bottom up to the high precipice. Myriads of angels holding swords, with wings they crowned it like a cloud. And afterwards our Lady Mary, pure and Virgin, the Mother of God, spoke to him and said to the elect abba Malke'a Krestos: "In its time, the angels and the saints of Wali will incense your tomb for you - who ride the clouds in the guise of elephants and lions and leopards - until this time is finished and until Christ comes. And now introduce the dust of the hermitage of Wali inside Dabra Kidana Mehrat Tekuz, that which the disciples of the saints have brought for you; since they have brought this dust for you, the time of your rest is completed."
ወዘንተ ፡፥ ብሂላ ፡ ወረወት ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ እዴሃ ። ወተተከለ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ዘብርሃን ፡ ማዕከለ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ዘቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ዘተከላ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለደ ብረ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ትኩዝ ፡ ወዘሰመያ ፡ ገነተ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ በረከተ ፡ እከዑ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ለለዕለቱ ፡ ወለለሰዓቱ ፡ ወአነኒ ፡ ኢይርኅቅ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ለትከኩዝ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ደብረ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወዘልፈ ፡ እሄውፃ ። ወለለዕለቱ ፡ ዘይጹልዩ ፡ በውስ ቴታ ፡ እትዌከፍ ፡ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወእሰሪ ፡ ኃጢአቶሙ ። ወአበዝጎኅ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ወአባ ርክ ፡፣ ለውሉዶሙ ። ወአበዝኅ ፡ ጥሪቶሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ትኩዝ ። ወለ”ካህናቲሃኒ ፡ አምዕዝ ፡ መሥዋዕቶሙ ፡ ወአፄኑ ፡ መዳዓዛ ፡ መአዛ ፥ ሠናየ ። ወለመነኮሳትሂ ፡ እትዌከፍ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ቀኖናሆሙ ። ወአአትት ፡ ኀዘኖሙ ፡ ወሕማሞሙ ፡ ረለ5አለ ፡ ይተግሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ አናቅጺሃ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌ-ሊ-ተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ቂም ፡ ወፀብዕ ፡ ወሐማት ፡ ወነገረ ፡ ውዴት ። ወነገረ ፡ ጋዕዝ ፡ ዳዕሙ ፡ አለ ፡ ይሄልዉ ፡ በፍቅር ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ወተደመ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ እግዝ እትየ ፡ ኢያስቄረረኪኑ ፡ ኃጣውየ ፡ ዘመጠነዝ ፡ ከብረ ፡ ዘወሀብኪኒ ፡ በኢድልወትየ ። ወሰገደ ፡ ቅድሜሃ ፡ ምንት ፡ ይትነገር ፡ ከብር ፡ ዘተውህበ ፡ ሎቱ ። አሜሃ ፡ ለመል
Dabra Kidana Mehrat Tekuz, called Gannata 61 And having said this, she threw the cross that she had in her hand, and this cross of light fixed itself in the middle of the altar of the sanctuary that he had planted with his hands, Dabra Kidana Mehrat Tekuz, and which he called Gannata. And she said to him: "I will scatter many blessings upon it every day and every hour, and I will not depart from Tekuz, the holy Dabra Maryam, and I will always provide for it and every day I will welcome the prayer of those who will pray in it and I will forgive their sins and I will multiply their food and I will bless their children and I will multiply the goods of the men of this Dabra Maryam Tekuz, and furthermore I will aromatize the sacrifices of its priests and I will scatter a sweet odor. And I will also welcome promptly the penance of its monks; and I will remove the sadness and the suffering of those who watch at its gates day and night, without rancor and war and outrage and speech of slander and speech of contention, only of those who live with love." And having heard this, he marveled and was astonished and said to her: "My lady, do you not perhaps detest my sins, for having given me so much glory in my unworthiness?". And he prostrated himself before her. How can the honor that was given then to Malke'a Krestos be told? | the five disciples "pillars of the church"

62 And after this he called his children: abba Amda Berhan and abba Zamanfas Qeddus and abba Gannata Maryam and abba Newaya Maryam and abba Sehina Maryam, who were the five pillars of the church. And he told them everything that our Lady had said to him and that she had done. And when his children heard this, they marveled and were astonished, and for the much joy they praised the Lord their God and gave thanks to our Lady pure and doubly Virgin Mary, Mother of God. And he said to them: "Scatter the dust of Waldebba, to the right and left, in front and behind this place, Dabra Maryam Tekuz, so that it may be sanctified." And they did as he had ordered them, distributing it with their tunic, and they scattered it over it and over its branches, which are the following: (one) Ad Kesad and the second Ad Qayahti and the third Makram and the fourth Ad TeSum, from Qalaya Asat to Me'sara Anabest which are named like Waldebba. And all the monks, children of abba Malke'a Krestos, arrived, and they loved (the monastery) much, while they said: "Its grass is green and also its trees (are) green and furthermore its waters are abundant and also its land, everything of it is good: what is better than its beauty?". And he loved Dabra Kidana Mehrat Tekuz, more than all the monasteries, and they built there a small seat for the tabot and for the dwelling of the community. And no one knew them, since it was countryside.


[p. 330] {#po.51.p330}

The governor of Walqayt 63 And, while they were living thus together, the governor of Walqayt and of Dagade named Walta came, while he was looking for the place of abba Malke'a Krestos; and when he found him, he carried a stone on his head and fell at his feet and said: "Have mercy on me, abba, for what I have done of evil towards you and towards the tabot of our Lady Mary Mother of God. Do you not know, my father, how the region of Walqayt is devastated by hunger and by the disease of the plague, and the elephants and the mice have invaded it? And now, abba, return to your places that you built with your hands; and if you and your tabot do not return, we will not find mercy." And abba Malke'a Krestos, meek more than all men, having heard this from his mouth, wept much.
ወአውሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ይመስለከኑ ፡ በፈቃደየ ፡ ዘአሐውር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ለአምላኪየ ፡ ቴር ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ተመየጥ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔርከ ፡ ወአውሥኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፥ መኩንን ፡ ኢየሐው”ር ፡ ብሔርየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ትሁር ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ አንተ ። ወዘእንበለ ፡ ታቦትከ ፡ ኢየአከለኒኑ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ምከንያተ ፡ ለንግደትየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እብጻሕ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘተአዘዘት ፡ ሊተ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ ተዓየነ ፡ መኩንን ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምዕ ራብ ፡ ወተከለ ፡ ሐይመታቲሁ ፡ ወደባትሪሁ ። ወአአኦደ ፡ ዓዋዴ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል(ፉን ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ እለ ፡ ትሴፅሩ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ ወ ፥ን ወአለ ፡ ተዓፅዱ ፡ ሣዕረ ። ወአንስ ትኒ ፡ ኢይቅድሃ ፡ ማየ ፡ አምዛቲ ፡ ፈለግ ። ወፈቀደ ፡ ይኅንፅ ፡ አረፍተ ፡ ዘዕብን ።
64 And our father answered him and said to him: "Do you perhaps think that I came by my will, without the will of my good God? And now return in peace to your country." And that governor answered him: "I will not go to my country without you coming with me and without your tabot: is my sin of before not enough for me?". And he answered and said to him: "As for you, you were the occasion of my pilgrimage, so that I might reach this place that had been ordered to me." And, having heard this, the governor camped towards the west and pitched his huts and his tents, and sent around a crier who said: "(Do not draw) from this river, you who cut the grass and who cut the grass; and also the women will not draw water from this river." And he wanted to build a stone wall. The governor of Tegray

65 And the governor of Tegray heard this and said: "Why has he come into my district of Ambasanayti?". And he sent word to the governor of Salawa and Enderta and Agame and Tar'a and Hamasén and Sarawe and Sere and Tamban and of all the provinces: "Into my district has come one from afar, whom I do not know: arise, come to help me." And, having said this, he gathered them promptly over that hermitage. And abba Malke'a Krestos knew it and prayed to his God, carrying a stone on his head and twisting his mouth, and he said: "Do not let me see the shedding of the blood of the Christians." And after having finished his prayer, he sent word to the two governors: "I beseech you not to kill each other, until I have come and have examined your question." And the governor of Ambasanit sent (word): "Have I perhaps come into his district? He instead has come into my district." And he sent word: "Have I perhaps taken away your goods and have I perhaps come to govern your district without water and grass and wood? What have I taken from you, that you have come to me to fight me? And now arise, be patient a little with me, until I have built

ከርስቲያን ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ሶበሰ ፡፥ አፍቀረተከ ፡ ታቦት ፡ እምነበ ረት ፡ በሐገርከ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ አነ ፡ አሐንፅ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለአቡየ ። ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ማነኮ ሳት ። ወናሁ ፡ ሑር ፡ በሰላም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገርከ ።
the church for my father." And he answered and said: "If he had loved you, there would have been a tabot in your city: and now I will build for my father and for all the monks. And behold, go in peace to your city." Malke'a Krestos makes peace between the governor of Walqayt and that of

### Tegray

66 And in this way our father dismissed them and sent them both away. And he said to the governor of Walqayt: "Go to your city and guard the hermitages of Wali for its monks; and behave well and do not sin again, so that your city may be blessed; indeed food will not be lacking in your city." And after having said "yes", he went on his way in peace and re-entered his district. And the other instead camped towards the east of that hermitage and pitched his tents and planted and made the pavilions and gathered his troops. And afterwards, on foot, without horse or mule, he went down to our father Malke'a Krestos and where our Lady Mary was, who is called Kidana Mehrat, and fell at the feet of our father Malke'a Krestos and said to him: "What will I do, my father, for the city in which you live and for the tabot? Do you want or do you not want me to build for you, or that I return to my city?". And our father answered him and said to him: "If your heart wants, build; and let not my city be where a herald goes around, saying: 'The men of the city who had been driven out and those who were dispersed throughout the territory have returned.' And you therefore do not be negligent." And, having heard this, he rejoiced much, since he feared to speak before him. And he prostrated himself on the ground and kissed his feet and (said): "Bless me and bless my city and my land." The saint blesses the governor of Tegray
ወአቡነሂ ፡ ባረኮ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ቡርከተ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ሀገርከ ፡ ወቡሩከ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ቤትከ ፡ ወይኩኑ ፡ ቡሩካነ ፡ ዕፀዊሃ ፡ ወማያቲሃ ፡ በሀገርከ ። ወይኩን ፡ ቡሩከ ፡፥ ማፅዕረራ ፡ ወሣዕራ ። ወአንተኒ ፡ ኩን ፡ ቡሩከ ። ወቡሩካነ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ውሉ ድከ-ሙ>» ፡ ወኢይጥፋዕ ፡ ዘርፅ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፥፡ መንበርከ ። ወተባሪኮ ፡ አፆዖደ ፡ ዓዋዲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ተመየጡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ እለ ፡ ተዘረውከሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሀገር ። ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ወሣዕረ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ከር ስቲያን ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ዘይብልዋ ፡ ደብረ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ትኩዝ ። ወካልዕታ ፡ ጸፍጸፍ ። ወፈጸመ ፡ ሐኒፆታ ። ወሐነፀ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መነኮ ሳት ፥ ወቤተ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ሠርአ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ጐልታቲሃ ፡ ለአድ ፡ ከሳድ ፡ ወለአድ ፡ ቀያህቲ ፣ ለመከረም ፡ ወአድ ፡ ትሹም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩና ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወለካህ ናት ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ወለተዝካረ ፡ በዓላቲሃ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገር ። ወይቤ ፡ ዕፀዊሃኒ ፡ ወሣዕራኒ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ለታቦተ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ተኩዝ ፡ ወኢይባዕ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ተሀቢሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ጸባሕተ ፡ አምኔሃ ። ወትኩን ፡ ለካህናት ፡ ወለመነኮሳት ፡ ለባሕቲቶሙ ።
67 And our father blessed him, saying: "Be blessed your city and be blessed your house and be blessed its plants and the waters in your city; and be blessed its harvests and the grass; and be blessed also you and be blessed your children, and may the good lineage that, after you, will sit on your seat not be extinguished." And having been blessed, the crier went around, saying: "The men whom you had dispersed throughout the region have returned to the country." And he gathered the wood and the grass for the church which is Dabra Maryam, which they called Dabra Madhanit Tekuz, and second Safsaf; and he finished building it. And he also built the house of the monks and the house of the community, he arranged and ordered its branches: Ad Kesad and Ad Qayahti, Makram and Ad Tesum, so that they might be sustenance for the monks and the priests who live there and for the commemoration of its feasts. And he ordered again for the city and said: "Its woods and its grass be for the tabot of Kidana Mehrat Tekuz; and the governor shall not enter it, having the audacity to take tribute from it, (which) will be only for the priests and for the monks."


[p. 334] {#po.51.p334}
ወአምድኅረ ፡ ሠርዓ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴ ብሕ ። ወሀገርኒ ፡ ዘማሰነት ፡ ተንሥአት ፡ እምድቀታ ፡ ወተሐንፀት ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወዛህን ፡ ላዕሌሃ ። ወአቡነሂ ፡ ምዕረ ፡ ይነብር ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ይጹሊ ። ወም ዕረ ፡ ይትነሣእ ። ወየሐውር ፡ ርጎቀ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ያእምሮ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይገብፅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኑ ። ወደቂቁኒ ፡ ተከሉ ፡ አውያነ ። ወመንፈቆሙ ፡ ተገበሩ ፡ ምድረ ። ወዘርኡ ፡ ገራውሃ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ማዕረረ ፡ ዕከል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢያጸርዑ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ዘሠርዑ ፡ አበው ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ አድኅነኒ ፡ አምዕረፍተ ፡ ሥጋ ። ወኢ ትኩን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኩን ። ወቦ ፡ ዘይገዝም ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ወይሴፅር ። ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ የሐ ርፁ ። ወቦ ፥፡ እለ ፡ ይፀውሩ ፡ ማየ ። ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ይተግሁ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ከርስቲያን ፡ በዕጣን ፡ ወፉርባን ። ወከመዝ ፡ ይትማሠጥዎ ፡ ለትሩፋት ፡ ወለአናግድሂ ፡ ይትቄበል ፆሙ ፡ እምርሁቅ ። በረከቱ ፡ ወረድኤቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ወፍቅርቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ለዓለመ ፣ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፳ ፳
68 And afterwards, he ordered them to return all to their own place, while he exulted. And the city of Masanat rose from its destruction and was rebuilt promptly, and in it there was peace and tranquility. And our father once stayed there to pray and once he arose and went into a distant region, without anyone knowing it, and returned again to his seat. And his children planted vineyards, and half of them cultivated the land; and they sowed fields and produced harvests of grain, while they did not neglect the prayer that the fathers had ordered. As the book said: "Free me from the rest of the body, and let it not be without work." And some cut the wood and split (it) and some ground and some carried water and some attended to the incense and the Eucharist for the church. And thus they competed to snatch merits and welcomed the guests (who came) from afar. The blessing and the help of our father Malke'a Krestos be with his servant, our father Gabra Sellase and the beloved of our father Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen! First miracle: meeting of Malke'a Krestos with Walatta Seyon
ንግባዕኬ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መን-ከ-ራቲሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ። ወንዜኑ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ተአምራቲሁ ፡ ዘተገብረ ፡ በበመካኑ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ሴዋ ፡ መጺኦ ፡ እምድረ ፡ ዋግዳ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ እምድረ ፡ ሪቅ ። ወአሐተ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ በመኃትወ ፡ ፆመ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ወሀበቶ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ለመልክዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ተር ሙሰ ፡ ፪መስፈርተ ፡ ቃቡ ፡ ወማየ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ በሐምሐም ። ወለሊሃ ፡ ነሥአት ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ወተሰነአለቶ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ተሰነአላ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ወጾመ ፡ ጓመዓልተ ፡ ወ ጓሌሴሊተ ፡ ሰሙነ ፡ ሕርቃል ፡ ወሰሙነ ፡ ሕማማት ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ኢጥ ፅመ ፡ እከለ ፡ ወኢሰትየ ፡ ማየ ። ወበመሐትወ ፡ ፋሲካ ፡፣ ገብአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እሙ ፥፡ ወለተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ወወሀባ ፡ ተርሙሰ ፡ ወማየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ወሀ.በ›ቶ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ለሊሃ ። ወይእቲኒ ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ዘነሥአት ፡ እስመ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ መሐላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይን ግራ ፡ ዘኮነ ። ወተዜያነዉ ፡ ዕበያቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።
69 Let us return, then, to the wonders of our father Malke'a Krestos and narrate all the miracles he performed in each of his residences. While he was in the land of Shewa, having come from the land of Wagda, he entered from the land of Rige. One day, on the eve of our Lord's fast, the holy Walatta Seyon gave Malke'a Krestos two measures of lupins and a little water in a gourd; she also took some for herself, and she greeted him. He greeted her in return and entered his cave and fasted for forty days and forty nights, the week of Heraclius and the week of the Passion, so that he tasted no food and drank no water. On the eve of Easter, he returned to his (spiritual) mother Walatta Seyon and gave her the lupins and the water, just as she had given them to him previously. She then showed him what she had taken, for they had sworn that he would tell her what would happen. And they recounted the magnificences of the Lord.
ወእምዝ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ከርስቲያን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ በድኅ ሬሁ ፥ እስከ ፡ ትመጽእ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ሰሪሆ ፡ እደዊሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይት መጠው ፡ ሞርባነ ። ወየበውዕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጾማዕቱ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ግሼቅ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ረኀበ ፡ ዘይበጽሖ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቀቢፀ ፡ ተስፋ ። ወጸለየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ለምንት ፡ ይትቃወመኒ ፥ ዝኩ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ርኩስ ። ወጸለየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዝእ ትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወለሊሃ ፡ ነገረቶ ፥ ዘንተ ፡ ለፍቁር ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፥ ለምንት ፡ ይትቃወሞ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በረኃብ ፡ ወበጽምዕ ፡ ይመከሮ ።
70 And afterwards he entered the church while the sun was behind him, until it came before him and he stood with his hands outstretched, and then he received the Eucharist. He would enter the caves; and when he was in the hermitage of Gesaq, one day, while he was praying, Satan provoked in him a great hunger that caused his hope (to resist) to fail. He prayed to his God, saying: "Why does this unclean Satan oppose me?". And he prayed again to our Lady Mary, and she reported this to her beloved son Jesus Christ, saying: "Why does Satan oppose him with hunger and tempt him with thirst?". And after this, our Lord descended, having


[p. 336] {#po.51.p336}
ወአምድኅረዝ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እግዚእነ ። እሂዞ ፥ ኅብስተ ፡ ሰማያዌ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ለአ ቡነ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ተሲስዮ ፡ ፀንዓ ፡ ወቀን”ፀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኃያል ። ወሞዖ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወትዘርዉ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ከመ ፡ ጢስ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይዘርዉ ፡፣፡ ፀሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ወዓመቱ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፳
taken heavenly bread, and gave it to our father Malke'a Krestos who, having been satisfied by it, was strengthened and leaped like a valiant man. He overcame Satan, and the Satans dispersed like smoke, and may the enemy of our father Gabra Sellase and of his handmaid Walatta Sellase be dispersed in the same way, for ever and ever, amen! Second miracle: institution of the hermitage of Gesaq
ወአመ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ግሽቅ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀፒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተጋብዑ ፡ አንስቲያሆሙ ፥ ወውሉዶሙ ። ወእንስሳሆሙ ። ወሐነፁ ፡ አብ ያተ ፡ በጥቃሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እገዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ይነሥኡነኑ ፡ ርስተ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ወአምሔወዉነ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፈይትውያን ፡ ዘኢይትአመር ፡ ሀገሮሙ ። ወአቡነሂ ፡ ተዓገሶሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅ ድሁ ፡፥ ማየ ፡ እምንስቲት ፡ ነቅዕ ፡ ዘትሰማይ ፡ አጋም ፡ ውኃ ። እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ካልዕ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ባሽሎ ፡ ወምንጭቅ ። ወረከብዎን ፡ ለአንስት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀድኃ ፡ ማየ ። ወይቤልዎን ፡ ለምንት ፡ መጻእከን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝየ ። ወአውሥ ኣሆሙ ፡ ወይቤላሆሙ ፡ ርስተ ፡ አቡከሙኑ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ እምከሙ ። ወማየሂ ፡ ኢንሁ በከሙ ፥ እምይእዜ ። ወተመየጡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ እገዘ ፡ ኢይቀድሁ ፡ ማየ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለምን-ት> ፡ አምጻእከነ ፡ በውስ”ተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ መካን ፡፥ ኅበ ፡ ሀለዋ ፡ እኩ ያት ፡ አንስት ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ይነብር ፡ እሳት ፡ ማዕከለ ፡ ማይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይጠፍዕ ፡ ወቦኑ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ምስለ ፣፡ እሳት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይውዒ ። ወከማሁ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ይከሉኑ ፡ ነቢር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አንስት ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘርእያ ፡ ለብእሲት ፡ ወፈተዋ ፡ ወድዓ ፡ ዘመወ ፡ በልቡ ። ወበከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጳው ሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፥ ይሔይሶ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ኢቀሪበ ፡ አንስት ። ወአቡነሂ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ሎሙ ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ ተዓግሠ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ይኤዝዝ ፡ ለነ ፡
71 And when he entered the hermitage of Gesaq, the men of that region came, gathering their wives and their children and their livestock, and they built dwellings in the vicinity of that hermitage, saying: "Those marauders, whose country is unknown (and) who have uprooted us, have they perhaps taken from us the inheritance of our father and our mother?". And our father was patient with them. And afterwards, the children of the chosen abba Malke'a Krestos drew water from a small spring called Agam Weha, since there was no other water except the Baselo and Mentegq rivers. They found some women while they were drawing water, and they said to them: "Why do you come here?". And they answered and said to them: "Is it perhaps the fief of your father and the fief of your mother? And from now on we will not give you water anymore". They returned to their father without having drawn the water, and they said to him: "Why have you led us to this place where there are bad women? Can fire perhaps stay in the midst of water without being extinguished, and can hay stay with fire without burning? And in the same way, can monks dwell with women? As our Lord said in the Gospel: 'Whoever looks at a woman and desires her, has already committed adultery with her in his heart' (Mt 5:28). And as the apostle Paul said: 'It is good for a man not to be near women' (1 Cor 7:1)". And so our father also said to them: "My children, be patient a little, if it ever happens for them, that He orders us (to go to) another place". Malke'a Krestos provides water for his monks

72 And, having said this, he moved away from them to pray, and he prayed while emitting moisture from his mouth. And praying thus, he said: "You who made water flow from the rock for Israel, by the work of your servant Moses (Num 20:11), so that his multitudes might quench their thirst, grant now water to my children so that they may drink". And while he was praying thus, a sweet water gushed forth in the midst of the rock, and if they drew it, there was none that resembled it. And it continued for thirty days without changing its taste. The other water, however, if they drew it and carried it, after seven days, it would spoil and its taste would alter. And afterwards his children drank and rejoiced and called it "Zamamher weha", which means 'water of the abba'. And, for the men of the country, there was a calamity, even for their wives and their children and for their livestock. And those who had remained dispersed; and those who had died

were not buried, since there was no one to bury them. And one day he remained until the setting of the sun while he was still instructing his children. And his children said to him: "Abba, if you remain, speak to us, and if you do not remain with us, arise: we will take leave of you". And our father said to them: "I walk cautiously, you instead would have difficulty, since the road of this place is bad". And while they were speaking thus, he immediately went up (moving away) from them; and his children saw him remaining on high, on the mountain: they were amazed and astonished, and they could not speak because of the great tribulation. Third miracle: prediction of the death of Queen Sabla Wangel

73 And again we narrate how Queen Sabla Wangel came from the land of Dawent, and said to him: "Abba, tell me how I shall save my soul". And our father answered and said to her: "Distribute your goods to the poor and the destitute, to the widows and the orphans". And she did as he had ordered her, before abba Malke'a Krestos, and gave to the poor many goods, even her garments, and then she received communion at the hand of our father, and returned to her house and remained in peace. And our father knew by grace the time of her death and sent his disciples, saying: "Ask about my daughter Sabla Wangel, for a grave illness has struck her". And his children went and found her while she was lying down due to the infirmity, and afterwards they returned to their father and narrated to him all that had happened. And at the moment of her departure, he gathered his children and went up into the land of Dawent, having known by grace the time of her death without anyone having told him. And he buried her with songs of joy and with psalmody and with hymns; and afterwards he returned to his hermitage. May his blessing and his help be upon his servant Gabra Sellase and his handmaid Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen. Fourth miracle: he helps a widow of Wadla
ወካዕበ ፡ ንንግር ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በምድረ ፡ የደጃ ፡ ነጸረ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ርኅብተ ፡ መበለተ ፡ ዘትነብር ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ዋድላ ። ወአልባ'ቲ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወትብል ፡ ርደአኒ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መል ከዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ጸውዓ ፡ ፪ተ ፡ ደቂቀ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሑሩ ፡ እገዘ ፡ አክለ ፡ ትፀውሩ ። ወሀብዋ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትሙት ፡ በረኃብ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ እፎ ፡ ነዓዱ ፡ ፈለገ ፥ በሽሎ ፡ ወፐጫ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ዘአዕደዎሙ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ ያዓድወከሙ ። ይእዜ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ተንሥኡ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ወዓደዉ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ጃጣ ፡ ወበሽሎ ፡
74 And again we narrate a miracle (that) the Lord (performed) for him. And while our father was in the land of Yadada, he saw in the Holy Spirit a hungry widow who lived in the city of Wadla. And she did not have daily food and was saying: "Help me, O God of abba Malke'a Krestos". And he called two children and said to them: "Go, carrying food, and give it to her so that she does not die of hunger". And they said to him: "How shall we cross the Baselo and Zota rivers?". And he said to them: "He who made the Israelites cross, will make you cross; and now depart immediately". And afterwards they went and crossed the Gata and Baselo rivers in the month of Nahase at the hour of midnight, and they knocked at her door and she

በወርኃ ፡ ነሐሴ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ። ወጐድጐዱ ፡ ጥኅታ ፡ ወአማዕተበት ፡ ገጻ ፥ በትአምርተ ፡ መስቀል ። ወይቤልዋ ፥፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከር ስቶስ ፡ ንሕነ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተወከፈቶሙ ። ወእሙንቱ ፡ ወሀብዋ ፡ ዘነሥኡ ፡ ወተዜያ ነዉ ፡ ርኅራነሁ ፡ ወይዋሄሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡፥ ወተመይጡ ፡ ጊዜ ፥ ነግህ ፥ ኀበ ፡ አቡሆሙ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ለነኒ ፡ የሀበነ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወነፍስ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ተማኅፀነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
signed her face with the sign of the cross, and those children of abba Malke'a Krestos said to her: "(It is) we". And then she welcomed them; and they then gave her what they had brought and narrated to her the goodness and the meekness of our father Malke'a Krestos; and in the morning they returned to their father. And so may He grant the nourishment of the soul and the body also to us, who entrust ourselves to the prayer of our father Malke'a Krestos, for ever and ever, amen! Fifth miracle: Malke'a Krestos obtains from the Lord the removal of a famine
ወናሁ ፡ ንንግር ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አምላኩ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተግ ህሠ ፡ እምደቂቁ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ፍጽምተ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ በብሔሩ ፡ ወጸንፕዓ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ገዳማቲሁ ፡ ተመንደቡ ፡ በረኃብ ። ወጸለየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ረኃበ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ በእንተ ፥ ደቂቁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እደወ(ኒ ፡ ተስዕለኒ ፡ ፍቁ ርየ ፥ ወይቤሎ ፡ እምይእዜሰ ፡ ኢይበውዕ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳማቲከ ። ወኅበ ፡ ዘተማኅፀነ ፥ በጸሎትከ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፳ = ፳
75 And behold, we narrate a miracle that his God performed for him. And after he had moved away from his children (for) a whole year, there was a famine in his region and it raged greatly, and all his hermitages were struck by hunger. And he prayed for the hunger of the people, and even more for his children. And our Lord said to him: "My help has been asked for, my beloved - and He said to him: - from now on hunger will no longer enter your hermitages nor where they will entrust themselves to your prayer, for ever and ever, amen!". Sixth miracle: meekness of the saint towards men and animals
ወእምዝ ፡ ንነግር ፡ ርኅራዔሁ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡፥ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኅድጉሰ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ለአራዊተ ፡ ገዳምኒ ፡ ይትራኅራኅ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ በውስተ ፡ በድው ፡ መጽአት ፡ ወይጠል ፡ ወቦአት ፡ ማዕከለ ፡ ብረኪሁ ፡ ወሠወራ ፡ በአድፍ ። እስመ ፡ ፈርሃቶሙ ፡ ለነዓውት ፡ ወለከለባት ። ወእምዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዴግ ንዋ ፡፥ በጽሑ ፡ ነዓውት ፡ ወከለባት ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ኢርኢከኑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተኃ ልፍ ፡ ወይጠል ። እስመ ፡ መሰሎሙ ፡ ዘይገዝም ፡ ዕፀ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኃለፈት ፡ በቅድሜከሙ ። ወፈነዋ ፡ እምድኅረ ፥ ኃለፉ ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ነጸረ ፡ ሆባይተ ፡ ርኅብተ ፡ ዘተፈ”ልጠት ፡ እምአዝማዲሃ ። ወይ ቤላ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡ ተፈለጥኪ ፡ እምአዝማድኪ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ድካምኪ ፡ አስመ ፡ ርኅብኩ ። ወሀሎ ፡ በጥቃሃ ፡ እከል ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ቃቤላ ፡ ወየዓቅባ ፡ ጳው ሎስ ፡ ወልዱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ሑር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ካሀ ፡ አነ ፡፥ አዓቅብ ፡ ለከ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ሖረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ተሴሰዩዬ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትፀግቢ ። ወፁሪ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትከሊ ። ወእምዝ ፡ በልዓት ፡ ወፀግበት ። ወእንዘ ፡ ትፀውር ፡ ሲሰያ ፡ ሖረት ። ወሶበ ፡ ነጸሩ ፡ ደቂቁ ። ከመ ፡ በልዑ ፡ ሆባያት ፡ ፃማሆሙ ፡ ቀተሉ ፡
76 And after (this), we shall narrate the meekness of Malke'a Krestos; he was moved to pity not only for men, but also for the wild beasts of the desert. One day, while he was praying in the desert, a wild goat came and inserted itself between his knees and he covered it with his tunic, for it was afraid of the hunters and the dogs. And afterwards, while they were chasing it, the hunters and the dogs arrived, and they said to our father: "Have you not seen a wild goat pass by?". For it seemed to them that he was cutting wood. And he said to them: "It has passed before you". And he sent it away after they had gone. And again, one day, while he was praying, he saw a hungry eagle that was separating from its flock. And he said to it: "Why have you separated from your flock?". And it said to him: "Because I am tired: in fact, I am hungry". And there was near there some grain called sorghum, and his son Paul was guarding it. And he said to Paul: "Go over there; I will watch for you". And he went as he had ordered him. And he said to that (eagle): "Eat until you are full, and carry what you can". And then it ate and was satisfied, and went away carrying its nourishment. And when his children saw that the eagles were eating (the fruit of) their labor, they killed seven eagles
፳ ሆባያተ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ኅዘነ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ቀተልከሙ ፡ ሆባ ያተ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ አማሰኑ ፡ እከልነ ፡ በፅንተዝ ፡ ቀተልናሆሙ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለሆባያት ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያማስኑ ፡ እከለ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ተቅራም ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያ1ን)ም-ጽ›ኡ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ተቅራሙ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡
77 And having seen this, he was saddened and said to his children: "Why have you killed the eagles?". And they said to him: "Because they consume our food: for this reason we killed them". And he ordered the eagles not to consume the grain, except for the gleanings only. And he ordered his children not to carry the gleanings with them, and it was so for many days. And one day, while he was praying, there came

to him our Lady, the holy doubly-Virgin Mary, Mother of God, while she was shining seven times more than the sun, and the angels followed her; and she sat upon a throne of light; and when our father saw her, he prostrated himself for fear and said to her: "My Lady, why have you come to your sinful servant? Do you not perhaps loathe my sins?". And she said to him: "It is for this humility of yours that I visit you always". And while she was saying this - they report - her son Walda Haymanot came to visit him. And when his father saw him, he signaled to him with his hand to turn back. And our Lady, the holy doubly-Virgin Mary, Mother of God, said to him: "Let him come to me, for he is a beloved of mine".


[p. 342] {#po.51.p342}
ኅቤየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፍቁርየ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእያ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ በድንጋፄ ። ወአእተተት ፡ ፍርሃተ ፡ እምላዕሴሁ ። ወባረከቶ ፡ በመስቀል ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ እዴሃ ። ወሚካኤልሰ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዘልፈ ፡ የአምር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ። ወሥልጣነ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ይትለአከ(ከን ፡ ወይትጋ ደል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውስተ ፥ ቤት ፡ ነጽሩኬ ፡ አአኃውየ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ከርስቲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘልፈ ፡ ትሔውጾሙ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ለአለ ፡ ያፈቅርዋ ፡ ወለእለ ፥ የሐንፁ ፡ መርጡላ ። ወለእለ ፡ ይትመሐፀኑ ፡ በኪዳና ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ይጹ ውዑ ፡ ስማ ። ወለእለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ተዝካራ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ወፍቅርታ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።፡ ሯ፳ ።ሯ ወንንግር ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፥ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በገ ዳመ ፡ ስለልኩላ ፥ ተግኅሠ ፡ እምደቂቁ ፡ እንዘ ፥ ይብል ፡ ኢተሐውጴኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይትፌጸም ፡ ወር(ዒንኃ ፡ ጾም ፡ ዘእግዚእነ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ፳ እምደቂቁ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሐ ውጾ ፥ ነሚኦ ፡ ፪ ጵርስፎራ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ እግዚአነ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ተዓቅፈ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ወመሠጦ ፡ መልአክ ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ለዘነሥአ ። ወወገረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓፅፉ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ እንዘ ፥ ይቴክዝ ። ወአምኃ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ወሰአቱኒ ፡ ሠርከ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፥ ምንተ ፡ ሐለይከ ፡ ዮም ፡ ወምንተ ፡ ገበርከ ። ወይቤ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘገ በርኩ ፡ አቡየ ። ወአርአዮ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጵርስፎራ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ መኑ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ለዝ ንቱ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ደንገፀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ወአንከረ ፡ ወተደመ ። ወይቤ ፡ እወ ፡ አባ ፡ አበስኩ ። ወኢያእመርኩ ፡ ዘመሰጠኒ ፡ እምእዴየ ። ወአንሰ ፡ እሔው ጸከ ፡ አአቡየ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ሕያው ፡፣ አንተ ። ወለእመ ፡ ምውት ፡ አንተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አምይእዜሰ ፡ ኢትድግም ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ነገረ ። ወንሣእ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ገብ”ዓ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አኃው ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዴመም ። አኦአኃውየ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ከርስቲያን ፡ እስፍ ንተ ፥ ንነግር ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡፥ መልከዓ ፡፥፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይበዝኅ ፡ ዘኢተጽሕፈ ፡ አምዘተጽሕፈ ፡ በረከቱ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ወፍቅርቱ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ሥላሴ ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፥ <አሜ›ን ፡ ፡= ፳፡
78 And when he saw her, he fell down for fright. And she removed the fear from him and blessed him with the cross that she had in her hand. As for his son Michael, he always knew everything that was happening, and the power of Christ was with him (who) served and struggled in the house. See then, O my brothers, Christian people, how our Lady Mary, Mother of God, always provides for those she loves and for those who build her sanctuary and for those who entrust themselves to her covenant and for those who invoke her name and for those who make her commemoration. May her prayer and her blessing be with her servant, our father Gabra Sellase, and his beloved Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen! Seventh miracle: the Eucharistic oblation 79 And we narrate again a miracle of Malke'a Krestos. And while he was in the hermitage of Salalkulla, he moved away from his children, saying: "Do not visit me until the month of our Lord's fast is finished". One of his children arose to visit him, carrying with him two oblations (of the mass), and while our Lord was going along the road, (the disciple) stumbled and fell, and an angel snatched from his hand what he had taken and threw it into the tunic of our father. And he then arrived where our father Malke'a Krestos was, while he was sad, and kissed our father on the forehead; and it was the hour of evening. And our father said to him: "What have you been occupied with today and what have you done?". And he said: "I have done nothing, my father". And he showed him that oblation. And he said to him: "Who brought this?". And having seen this, that disciple was frightened and was amazed and astonished and said: "Yes, abba, I have sinned and I do not know who snatched (the oblation) from my hands; as for me, I visited you, O my father (to know) if you are alive and if you are dead". And our father said to him: "From now on indeed do not repeat such speech; and take this". And afterwards he returned to the brothers, while he was astonished. O my brothers, Christian people, how many miracles of our father Malke'a Krestos shall we narrate? And what has not been written is greater than what has been written (Jn 20:30). May his blessing be with our father Gabra Sellase and his beloved Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen!
ወካዕበ ፣፡ ንዜኑ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወከቡር ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጾማዕቱ ፡ መጽኡ ፣ ፪መነኮሳት ፡ አኃው ። ለ፳ስሙ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ወለካረል»ዑ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ከር ስቶስ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ አምላኪየ ። ተንሥእ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወእሁከ ፡ ወሑሩ ፡፥ ውስተ ፡ ትዕይንተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከዓዉ ፡ ደመከሙ ፡ ወፈጽሙ ፡ ገድለከሙ ። ወለከሰ ፡፥ ኢይትከዓው ፡ ደምከ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ እግዚእነ ። ባርከነ ፡ አባ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሁር ። ወአቡነሂ ፡ ባረኮሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትዕይንት ፡ ወቆሙ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአውገዝዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ኢታውስብ ፡ ፪ተ ፡ አኃተ ። ወተምዓ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ይምትርዎሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ሰያፍ ያን ፡ ወመተርዎ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ለማኅደረ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ከሣዶ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተመትረ ፡ ከሣዱ ። ወጠነ ፡ ያንብብ ፡ መዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ። ወፈጸመ ፡ መኃልየ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ 1ወመኃልየ ፡ ነቢያት ።ን ወመሐልየ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወውዳሴ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እግዝእትነ ። ወድርሳነ ፡ ያሬድ ። ወእሎንተ ፡ ጸሎታተ ፡ ፈጺሞ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ወሳሙኤ ልሂ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ከሣዶ ፡ ለሰያፊ ፡ ከብርተ ፡ ከሣዶ ፡ ወተመ
Eighth miracle: the king frees the monks who accused him of having married two sisters 80 And again we narrate how Malke'a Krestos, great and glorious, went out to fight. And while he was in his cave, two monk brothers came: one by the name of Mahdara Krestos and the other Samuel. And Mahdara Krestos said to our father: "Thus my God said to me: ‘Arise, you and your brother, and go to the king's camp, shed your blood and complete your struggle. As for you, however - my Lord said to me - your blood will not be shed’. Bless us, abba, so that we may go". And our father blessed them. And afterwards they arrived at the camp and stood before the king and adjured the king, saying: "Do not marry two sisters". And he became angry, and ordered that they be cut down with the sword. And the executioners came and first cut the neck of Mahdara Krestos. And after his neck had been cut, he began to recite the Mazmura Dawid and finished the Mahaleya Nabiyat and the Mahaleya Salomon and the Weddase Maryam of our Lady, and the Dersana Yared. And having finished those prayers, his mouth stopped; and Samuel then, having seen all this, presented his glorious neck to the executioner; and his neck was cut, and milk came out from his mouth and from his neck.


[p. 344] {#po.51.p344}
ትረ ፡ ወውኀዘ ፡ ሐሊብ ፡ እምአፉሁ ፡ እምከሣዱ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ፳መማነኮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢያእመርከኑ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኃልቁ ፡ መነኮሳተ ፡ መጐና ፡ አባ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ዘይትማጠኖ ፡ ለጽድቁ ፡ ወለካልዓን ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ጐልሞ ። ቦ ፡ አለ ፡ ተቀትሉ ። ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ተመቅሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አረሚ ፡ ወበቤተ ፥፡ ፈላሹ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አግብርት ። ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ተሰዱ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ነደ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ከመ ፣ እሳት ። ወበከየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ለአከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መንኮረሳ-ተ ፡ ጋይንት ፡ ወሳይንት ፡ ወዋድላ ። ወላስታ ፡ ወሠሜጨ ፡ ወበጌም ድር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ንፃዕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሙትአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃዊነአ ። ወምንትኑአ ፡
81 And there came again a monk to our father and said to him: "Do you not know, my father, that the monks of Magwina abba Amha Giyorgis - whose righteousness is immense - and other countless monks have been exterminated? Some of them have been killed, and some have been put in chains in the quarter of the pagans and in the quarter of the Falasha and in the quarter of the slaves, and some of them have been driven away". And hearing this, our father, his heart burned like fire, and he wept a bitter weeping. And then he sent word to the monks of Gayent and of Sayent and of Wadla and of Lasta Sameéa and Bagemder: "Let us go out to die with our brothers. And what is our monasticism? And now let there be a meeting of ours in the land of Gayent". And having agreed,


[p. 346] {#po.51.p346}
ምን(ስንኩስናነአ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ይኩን ፡፥ ተጋብዖትነ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ጋይንት ። ወአሆ ፡ ብሂሎሙ ፡ ተጋብዑ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ጋይንት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተዓየኑ ፡ በመድረ ፡ ዴራ ። ወንጉሥ[ሠን ፡ ይነብር ፡ በምድረ ፡ አረሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ደንገፁ ፡ ወጸውዳ ፡ መማከርቲሁ ፥፡ ወመኳንንቲሁ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡ መጽኡ ፡ እሉ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ አለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ልሞ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ሰያፍያን ፡ ምንት ፡ ያዓፅበከ ። ወናሁ ፡ ንቀትሎሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማድነ ። ለ ፳ስሙ ፡ ከማል ፡ ሰኖ ። ወለ ፳ስሙ ፡
they gathered in the land of Gayent, and then they camped in the land of Dera. As for the king, he was in the land of Araha. And when they saw them, they were frightened, and (the king) called his counselors and his governors and said to them: "Why have those monks without number come?". And the executioners said to him: "What causes you difficulty? And behold: we will kill them as is our duty". One had the name Kamal Sano and one had the name Bassa Aygabaz. And those two powerful men were without pity.
ባሻ ፡ አይገበዝ ። ወእሉ ፡ ፪ቱ ፡ ኃያላን ፡ አለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ርኅራጌ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ እጨጌ ፡ ዘከርስቶስ ። ወይቤሎ ፥፣ ለነጉሥ ፡ ገሰ ፡ ነገር ፡ ኢይ ሜኒ ፡ ለነ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ንሕትቶሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘመጽኡ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ነገር ። ወንጉሥኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አሆ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ እጨጌ ፥ ዘክርስቶስ ። ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ በ፳ቃል ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሙት ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃዊነ ። እስመ ፡ አው ሰበ ፡ ፪ተ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ወአኃዊነ ። ወንህነኒ ፡ ንመውት ፡ ከማ ሆሙ ። ወግበሩነ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ትፈቅዱ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፍንዉ ፡ አበወየ ፡ ማፅምራነ ፡ ነገር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈንዉ ፡ አበውየ ፡ ማዕምራ-ረነ ፡= ነገር ፥ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፥ ወይሑሩ ፡ ምስሌየ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ለአከት ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኢትት.ኤደውአ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ኢትትአደውአ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስአ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ መነኮሳትአ ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኤአ ፡ ኅኀቤከአ ። ወለእመ ፡ ቀተልኮሙ ፡ ለእሉአ ፡ ኢቀውምአ ፡ መንገሥት"ከአ ፡ እሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ወእጨጌ ፡ ዘከርስቶስ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወነገሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘይቤልዎ ፡ መነኮሳት ። ወኃብረ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ ወቃለ ፡ እጨጌ ። ወይቤ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አይቴ ፡ ብሔሩ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ኃያል ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘይብልዎ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኃያል ፥ መነኮስ ፡ ተጋዳሊ ፡ በሃይማኖት ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይመስሎ ፡ አመነ
82 And the etage Za-Krestos arose and said to the king: "This affair is not good for us: first let us ask them why they have come for this affair". And then the king said yes. And afterwards the etage Za-Krestos arose, and they all went to the monks (affirming) with one voice: "Let us die with him, with our brothers. In fact, he has married two (sisters), and for this reason our fathers and our brothers died, and we too will die like them: do to us what you want!". And he said to them: "Send my fathers, who are knowledgeable of the matter, to the king". And that messenger said to him: "My fathers, who are knowledgeable of the matter to the king, let them come with me!". And while the king's envoy was still there, a queen sent word to the king: "Do not cast lots", and she repeated to him: "Do not cast lots against Malke'a Krestos and against the monks who have come to you. And if you kill those, your kingdom will not last an hour". And the etage Za-Krestos entered to the king and narrated to him everything that the monks had said to him. And the word of the queen and the word of the etage agreed. And the king said: "Where is the country of this powerful monk whom they call Malke'a Krestos?". And he said to him: "That powerful monk is a wrestler for the faith; and among the monks there is no one who resembles him".
ኮሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘተወድየ ፡ ፍርሃት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ። ወይቤ ፥ ለዘሞቱሰ ፡ ኢይከል ፡ አንሥአሙ ። ባሕቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘተሞቅሑ ፡ ይትፈትሁ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ተሰዱ ፥ ይትመየጡ ። ወመሐለ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እሉ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ምስ ሌሁ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ ዓዋዲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዘተዓሰረ ፡ ወዘተሰደ ፡ ይትመ የጥ ። ወዘተመዝበራ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ከርስቲያናት ፡ ይትሐነፃ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖሙ ፡ እሉ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ሖሩ ፥ ወነገርዎሙ ፡ ለአበዊሆሙ ። ወተበሃሉ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ በበይና ቲሆሙ ፡ እመ ፡ ኢከፈለነ ፡ ርስተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ንትኃነቅኑ ፡ በእዴነ ። አው ፡ ን“ትቀተለኑ ፡ ርእሰነ ፡ በእዴነ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይትባረኩ ፡ በአዴሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፥ ተመይጡ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወመነኮሳይያት ፡ ለለመካናቲሆሙ ፡ ወአቡነሂ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ። በረከቱ ፡
83 And when the king heard, fear entered his heart, and he said that it was not possible to resurrect those who had died, but he said that those who had been chained should be freed and that those who had been driven away should return. And he swore before all the men who were with him. And at that time a herald went out, proclaiming: "Whoever was driven away and whoever was exiled, let him return, and let the churches that have been destroyed be rebuilt". And, hearing this, those monks went and reported to their father. And the monks were affirming among themselves: "If we do not share the inheritance with the martyrs, shall we perhaps strangle ourselves with our own hands, or kill ourselves with our own hands?". And while they were blessed at the hand of our father Malke'a Krestos, the monks and the nuns returned each to their residences, and our father also returned to his place, while he wept for not having become a martyr. father Malke'a Krestos be with our father Gabra Sellase and his beloved Walatta Sellase and his father [empty space] and his mother [empty space], for ever and ever, amen! Ninth miracle: the saint predicts the time of the death of a magistrate 84 And again we narrate how our father Malke'a Krestos had told him at the time of the death of a man. And there was a magistrate in Bagemder, and he had sent word to him: "Do not forget me, my father". And our father also sent to him, saying: "Wash your garments and gird your loins and prepare your viaticum and set your house in order and be reconciled with all the men of your city and restore what you have defrauded, and after that receive the Eucharist. While you say: 'I will find it', so that the world does not find for you a prostitute whom you will love, but it happens that she hates you and that she joins with your neighbors. And your departure will be in the month of Teqemt". And, having heard this, the magistrate wept and did as he had ordered him. And he died in the month of Teqemt, as he had told him before. The blessing of our father Malke'a Krestos be with our father Gabra Sellase and with his beloved Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen! Tenth miracle: the saint saves a lion from the fall of a boulder 85 We narrate again a miracle of our father Malke'a Krestos; may his blessing be with his servant, our father Gabra Sellase and his beloved Walatta Sellase and his father [empty space] and his mother [empty space] and upon all the children of baptism, for ever and ever, amen! And while he was praying inside the hermitage of Tekuz, where there was the tabot of Kidana Mehrat which he had placed with his hands at the foot of a high mountain, a lion lay down under the feet of our father Malke'a Krestos and a large stone detached itself from the high mountain, without (the intervention of any) hand, as the prophet Daniel had seen (Dn 2:34). And the lion cried out and spoke with human tongue, and said: "Abba, abba, pray for me, so that this mountain does not destroy me". And our father Malke'a Krestos said: "Stay where you are, I adjure you!". And the stone stopped halfway down the precipice, without going up high and without going down low. And there is until now as a sign the footprint of the foot of our father Malke'a Krestos and of the lion: it is depicted on the rock. And so may the vigilant shepherd Malke'a Krestos save us too, when fear strikes us, and protect us from the punishment of the flood and pay attention to us with his great mercy. May he give us the blessing in the evening and in the morning; to us who have our garments dirty, may he purify us with his purity, and so may he save his servant, our father Gabra Sellase and his beloved Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen!

Eleventh miracle: the saint and the tabot of the Virgin Mary 86 And when he entered again into the land of Wadla, which is Salalkulla, he saw its inhabitants who were living without a tabot. And they said to him: "May our city not be depraved". And our father said to them: "Your city will not stand without the tabot". And furthermore he said to them: "Now indeed, my children, make the tabot, so that there may be peace in your country". And they answered him, saying: "Give us the tabot, for we, even if we seek the tabot, will not find (it) before. And behold, we will give to your children the third part of the land". And with such speech they agreed with our father. And after this, he gave them the tabot of our Lady Mary; and at the moment of midnight, our Lady, the holy doubly-Virgin Mary, Mother of God, appeared before our father Malke'a Krestos and said to him: "Why do you separate me from you? Had I not perhaps told you before not to separate me from you, and had I not sworn to you that I would not separate from you as you would not separate from me?". And, having heard this, our father gathered the men of the city in the morning, and said to them: "Give me, my brothers, my tabot". And they said to him: "We indeed agreed yesterday with you, and we do not give (it) to you". And our father said to them: "If you do not give it to me by your will, I myself will take it with my hands".

87 And while they were arguing about this, the leaders of the city came and said thus to the men of the city: "You give him his tabot and do not transgress his will". And they said to our father: "Give them another tabot and take your tabot as you have said, and you give him the fourth part of your land". And they agreed on this speech and their agreement remains until today. And our father then gave them the tabot of Michael the archangel. And he took the tabot that he had given before to them, and he did not separate from it until the day of his death, as our Lady, the holy Mary, Mother of God, had ordered him. And so may the help of our father not separate from us who trust in his prayer and in the time of her and in every day of his and may our life be with his life, and he, our father, may take care of us with his mercy and may his blessing be always upon us, for ever and ever, amen! Twelfth miracle: the saint orders his tomb to be built

gone, the men of the city will come, and they will go for you into this hermitage". And he said again to the first: "The men whom they call Benata have come and they will build the church for you". And he said to those two monks: "Order the men of Gar'alta and of the other cities that they honor the two Sabbaths, and that they practice the fasts and walk the straight path; and if they do all this, peace will increase in their city, otherwise their city will be destroyed". And afterwards, he gave them the tabotat and the furnishings of the house of the sanctuary and blessed for them the respective residences, and gave them some dust of Waldebba, and dismissed them and went on his way. And he said to them: "Build my tomb, if you do not precede (me)". And so may the Lord of our father Malke'a Krestos preserve us with his mercy; we entrust ourselves to the prayer with him, and we who are his, of our God, for he created us and not we. And at his second coming he will not shame us, and he will make us stand with his sheep that are at his right hand (Mt 25:33) and he will make us hear the voice of his blessing. And may he make his voice of joy and gladness heard to his servant, our father Gabra Sellase and to his beloved Walatta Sellase and to his father [empty space] and to his mother [empty space], for ever and ever, amen! Thirteenth miracle: Malke'a Krestos miraculously finds water

89 And again we narrate to you a miracle that God, our Lord and our Savior Jesus Christ, performed for him, for our father Malke'a Krestos. May his blessing be with our father Gabra Sellase and with his beloved Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen! And while he was praying, having gone up on a high hill of the land of Enteèéo, which is called Makram, his children separated to the right and to the left of that hill. And those on the right were leading the livestock and the camels and the donkeys that were loaded with the earth and the furnishings of the house of the sanctuary, and they arrived at the country of Ambasanit, which is called Terbo. He caught sight of that hermitage, and immediately they danced and leaped like a calf that has been untied from its tether and looks at its mother. And the monks were astonished and amazed, since the path on which they were dancing was not straight, while the animals were there. And our father, seeing this, fell at the foot of the precipice and prayed and drew the water. And his children drank the water and his animals also drank. And, as for those on the left, they could not go down since they did not find the road, and they remained where they were. grass, and, with great travail and affliction, they found themselves with those brothers of the right. And all the monks loved that (place) and wanted to build the hermitage there, and they gathered wood and grass. And our Lady Mary, Mother of God, appeared many times to our father Malke'a Krestos, saying: "This is not your hermitage, but for your hermitage". Construction of Dabra Saqwera 90 And after this, he sought the place where he built Dabra Saqwera. And his children also sought; and some of them went up on high mountains and others of them went up on a mountain in which there was a cave and some of them went up on a mountain that was split into two of its stone horns. And that one had moved away from the other, to manifest the power of the Lord, at the time of the departure of one of the saints. And some of them hid themselves in hidden caves and some went down into its abyss and prayed there, and some went with the tabot and the furnishings of the house of the sanctuary. As for our father, where he was in prayer, he was looking at a column of light, planted towards the east, and he thought in his heart and said: "Where is the column of light planted!". And the day after he passed beyond and arrived there and prayed in it and saw the great vision that there was over the city and over the hermitages and over the kings and the governors, and how in a first period there would be famine and the disease of the plague in all the cities, and how after there would be abundance and happiness for the prayer of our father Malke'a Krestos. 91 And (he saw) again that the wolves and the birds of prey would come - children of the heretic Leo - who would teach perverse things and would make (people) deviate from the law of the Lord. And how our blessed father, survivor of the prophets and follower of the apostles, would overcome them with his prayer, fighting for the straight faith. And he called his children who were near and said to them: "Pay attention that now the magistrates have come, saying: 'We will build for you'; they are good, but the last ones who had come, destroyed the church, since they were bad. Their lineage, however, has died with them; you instead and those who will come after you will be good for eight generations. But after them will come bad monks who will dispute for the (monastic) cap and who will wear precious garments and will love the glory of this passing world, and who will love gold and silver more than their soul and who will transform the word of the scriptures in their own manner and will not have the Holy Spirit and who will despise the teaching of the apostles which our Lord said to them: "Whoever listens to you listens to me, and whoever despises you, despises me and despises completely him who sent me' (Lk 10:16).

true one. Only that there are some who trouble you and want to pervert the doctrine of Christ. But as for you, you will follow our footsteps. And if (one of) you (or) an angel from heaven preached to you differently from what we have taught you, let him be anathema! And as I said before, now again I say: if anyone teaches you differently from what we have taught you and you have received it, let him be excommunicated!' (Gal 1:6-9). And the apostles said again: 'And whoever comes to you and does not bring this doctrine that is with you, do not let him enter your house and do not greet him even; for whoever greets him, associates himself with him in his bad work' (2 Jn 10)". And our father said to his children: "And the city then will promptly rise again; and the churches also will be rebuilt promptly. But afterwards, bad men will disturb them. And then indeed love and faith will diminish, as the prophet David said: 'Save me, for there is no longer a good (man) and faith has failed from men; they speak uselessly to one another. They speak with lying lips and with a double heart. May the Lord cut off the lying lips and the tongue that says arrogant things; those who say: We are great because of our tongues (Ps 11:1-3)'. And at that time the good will no longer be found, except for a few". The children of the heretic Nestorius

93 And our father said again to his children: "Beware that the children of Nestorius do not deceive you, the hyenas and the children of Leo, the wolves that tear the sheep, and do not leave the lambs, all those little ones, the foxes that devastate our vineyard (Ct 2:15). And they sow thorns on the ground; and the ground, having received from their hands, will make the thorns sprout and will make the thistles bloom (Heb 6:8). And they say thus: 'And when the Son of God met the Son of Mary the Virgin, he made him Son by grace'. And again the children of Leo pray for the single nature of the divinity. And they say that by the union with the flesh, there are two operations; that the divinity performs the work of the divinity and the humanity performs (the work) of the humanity. And for this reason our chosen apostle fathers said: 'Whoever does not declare the two (natures) united in power and in operating, let him be anathema'. And they say again: 'If anyone says that the glory in the Holy Spirit is like in one of the prophets or like in one of the apostles, let him be anathema'. And John Chrysostom says: 'If really his Son was superior to the angels' (Heb 1:4). And the prophet David for this said: "You anointed" (Ps 44:8), as a condition of the humanity; but he himself is the one who anoints his head with his Spirit only". And again our father said to his children: "Do not go out from the word of the scriptures". And afterwards indeed, in his country, the faith was rectified and love grew and peace came. And may the Lord of our father Malke'a Krestos make us also arrive at the straight faith and at that hour (Jn 16:4) and save us from sudden death. May his blessing be with all of us, children of baptism, for ever and ever, amen!

### Gadama Tekuz

94 And after he had said this to his children, all the brother monks gathered in that hermitage of Gadama Tekuz which had been sanctified by the hand of our father Malke'a Krestos so that it might be his dwelling. And there was no man who moved there, except for lions and leopards and rhinoceroses and other wild beasts, and except for the fish and the hippopotamuses that are in the waters. And there was no one among men and among the angels who knew them, until the governor of Walqayt came, he who had found them with great effort. And the governor of Ambasenit also came to fight that one. And afterwards the people knew that our father Malke'a Krestos and all the monks and also the tabotat were there. And the fame of our father spread to all the regions. May the intercession and the struggle and the tribulation of our father Malke'a Krestos protect our father Gabra Sellase and his servant Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen! Malke'a Krestos designates the seats of the monks, the priests, and the cantors

95 Let us return, therefore, to our first discourse and narrate how our father designated the places of the monks and the priests and the cantors, so that they would not be separated in prayer and would pray together according to their possibilities, and he strengthened this order for them. And other hermitages had also been built that were not his; and Abreha and Asbeha had built the sanctuary of our Lady Mary which is called Wegro. And he sanctified Ziba'ana with his hands. And there was the king who loved him and all the people loved him, since, at the time of his coming, the region, which was previously desolate, was blessed; and it remained for a long time in this situation, relying on the prayer of our father of sweet fame. And in his days it found peace and health and there was the plant of his faith, (like) a column of light, the intellect; and he crowned it with the blessing of the clouds. Thus may he crown his servant, our father Gabra Sellase and his servant Walatta Sellase, for ever and ever, amen! Spiritual testament of Malke'a Krestos 96 And when the day of the death of our father Malke'a Krestos came, eminent in works and in faith, he called his children and gathered them in one place and said to them: "Do everything that I have told you from the beginning until now, and be

strong from now on in my counsel; and be like me and follow in my footsteps. And love one another, as I told you before, since love extinguishes all sins (1 Pt 4:8); and welcome the pilgrim and the wayfarer and do not love the world, since our Lord told us in the gospel that his own love the world (Jn 1:10-11). And our father said again to his children: "Love your neighbor and love your enemies (Mt 5:44; Lk 6:27, 35), since thus said the Lord; do not go with the woman of another man and do not kill any soul; do not steal and do not bear (false) witness and do not covet (the goods of others), and (observe) also the rest that is in the commandments (Mk 10:19). And first of all (practice) the precept: 'Love your neighbor as yourself' (Mk 12:31); and whoever loves his neighbor does no evil. 'Love indeed is the fullness of the law' (Rm 13:10). And all this that I have told you, is not (only) for you, but so that you may tell it to those (who will come) after you. And leave sin and remember me in your prayer."

97 And all his children said to him: "Where are you going, our father, light of our eyes? You are our boast and our hope and you are our torch and you are our chariot with which we run; you are our boat that does not break and you are our sun that does not darken. And to whom will you leave us, as you told us? And will we not perhaps come with you? And did you not perhaps tell us not to divide (ourselves)?". And our father said to them: "Was I perhaps created with my hands? And now the great judge will come and will take one of your goods. But do not be saddened and do not be afflicted, since he who has taken and who has taken justly will not return to you, because he makes no distinction between persons, being a just judge". 98 And when his children heard this, they understood it in the Spirit, as he had spoken to them of his death, and they wept much weeping before him. And he said to them: "Why are you sad because of the good of others? Shall we perhaps force the great judge who comes with justice?". And his spiritual children said to him: "When will this great judge come who is clothed in righteousness, so that we may prepare ourselves for him and for his own?". And our father said to them: "Behold, his coming is near, for which he will send his envoy to us". And having therefore heard this, his children prepared a spacious refectory and prepared food and drink in abundance, since it seemed to them (that a multitude and people from the city would come), so that they prepared food and drink in abundance. In fact, it seemed to them that the king or one of the notables would come. Illness of the saint

consolation also increases" (2 Cor 1:5). And, while he was in such a situation, his children came, and, having seen him ill, they led him away immediately, weeping. As for our father, he comforted them and exhorted them with every good counsel; and they led him to the house of the community and laid him on a bed; and (the inhabitants of) the region heard that they had brought him and gathered promptly to visit their father, since they loved him very much. And the women indeed stood outside, since they could not enter inside the house of the community.

Last blessings of the saint to the disciples and the faithful 100 And, as for our father, he blessed them, saying: "May the Lord provide for you with his mercy and purify your soul and sanctify your body; may your children be blessed and may your women be blessed and may your prayer be pleasing. And may your word be accepted and may your livestock be blessed; blessed be your land, from Tekuz to all its branches; and blessed be the fruit of your land, blessed be your waters. And may the heart of the kings and governors be merciful to you, and may your path be prosperous; and (may the Lord) illuminate your hearts and make your children grow and prolong your days and multiply your food. And may he make milk and honey flow in your land, and may the Lord strengthen your faith, and may your counsel be upright, and may everything that will befall you be blessed. May you be blessed with the blessing of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit; may you be blessed with the blessing of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit; be blessed with the blessing of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit; with the blessing of our Lady, the holy doubly Virgin Mary, Mother of God. Be blessed with the blessing of the Four (celestial) Animals (Rev 4:6) and of the Twenty-Four Priests of heaven (Rev 4:4, 10) and of the eight terrible angels; be blessed with the blessing of Saint Michael the archangel and of Gabriel the announcer of joy; be blessed with the blessing of the principalities, of the ninety-nine hosts of angels; be blessed with the blessing of the thousands of thousands and of the myriads of myriads. 101 Be blessed with the blessing of Adam and of Abel, of Seth and of Enos and of Kenan and of Mahalalel and of Jared and of Methuselah (Gen 5); and the blessing of Abraham, Isaac, Jacob and of all the patriarchs; (be) blessed with the blessing of the prophets and of the apostles, with the blessing of the righteous and of the martyrs, with the blessing of the virgins and of the pious monks. Be blessed with the blessing of the holy church, the unique assembly of the apostles; with the blessing of the body and of the blood of our Lord Jesus

Christ. Be blessed with the blessing of the holy Sabbath of the Christians: be blessed". And our father, while he was blessing the monks and the people, sometimes stood upright and sometimes sat. And afterwards, our father said to his children: "Go to your homes". And as for the people who were inside Dabra Kidana Mehrat Tekuz, which had been sanctified by the hand of our father Malke'a Krestos, half of them guarded him, taking turns, and the other half went to their own homes, whereas the monks and the priests did not separate from him. The Virgin Mary visits the dying saint
