102 And with things being thus, all the brothers, the men of Tekuz, the queen of heaven and earth descended, the lady of all creation, Mary the Virgin, Mother of God, while her face illuminated the whole land of Tekuz seven times more than the sun, since she loved it much more than all the cities and hermitages. And she said to him: "O Malke'a Krestos, my beloved, I have come to you to establish with you the Covenant of Mercy that my Son gave me; with fasting you went out through the narrow gate (Mt 7:13) and you completed your days with prayer (Mt 17:20) and at no time did you let your body rest: now I have come to make you rest from the fatigue of this world". Jesus Christ with hosts of angels and saints comforts Malke'a Krestos 103 And our Lord Jesus Christ came with thousands of his angels and with the prophets and the apostles, the righteous and the martyrs, the virgins and the pious monks. And our Lord said with his voice to Malke'a Krestos: "My beloved and the beloved of my mother, choose the gifts that you want from me". And our father answered and said to him: "Bless, O Lord, my country, the land of Tekuz and of Makram and the land of Qayahti and of Alalu and of Ad Kesad and the land of Te$um, since I love them much more than all the countries. And bless, O Lord, this country and the other countries; and bless my hermitages and bless my children; have mercy, O Lord, on those who will make my commemoration and on those who will invoke my name and on those who will entrust themselves to my prayer".
ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መሐርኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘሰዓልከኒ ። ዘገ ብረ ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ ወዘጸውዓ ፡ ስመከ ፡ ወዘጸንዓ ፡ በትምህርትከ ፡ ወዘአጽሐፈ ፡ መጽ ሐፈ ፡ ገድልከ ። ወዘሐለየ ፡ ማኅሌተ ፡ በበዓልከ ፡ ወዘሐነፀ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ከርስቲያነ ፡ በእንቲአከ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ እምየ ። ወዘያበውዕ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ወማኅቶተ ፡ ለተዝካረ ፡ በዓ ልከ ። ወዘተማኅፀነ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፲ ወ ፳ ትውልድ ፡ እምህር ፡ ለከ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ተካየድኩ ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኢተዓደዉ ፡ ትእዛዝየ ፡ ወእምትአዛዝከ ፥ ኢይመ ውቱ ፡ በረኅብ ። ወአነሂ ፡ ኢያመጽእ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡፥ ንዴተ ፡ ወተፅናሰ ። ወአባርከ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ምድሮሙ ። ወለእመ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ። አአምላኩ ፡ ለመሙልከዓ ፡
104 And our Lord said to our father: "I will have mercy for you on all those whom you will ask of me, on whoever will make your commemoration and on whoever will invoke your name and on whoever will persevere in your teaching and on whoever will have the book of your life written; and on whoever will sing the hymn on your feast and who will build a church for you and for my mother; and on whoever will offer incense and lamps for the commemoration of your feast and on whoever will trust in your prayer, up to eleven generations, I will have mercy for you; thus I covenant with you; and if they do not transgress my commandment and your commandment, they will not die of hunger. And I will not bring indigence and misery upon them and I will bless the fruit of their land. And if they pray to me, saying: 'O God of Malke'a Krestos, help me', I will immediately answer (them); of the sinners, if they will be


[p. 366] {#po.51.p366}
ከርስቶስ ፡ ርድአኒ ፥ ፍጡነ ፥ እሰምዖሙ ፡ ኃጥዓንሂ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ተቀብሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ለገዳምከ ፡ ትኩዝ ፡ አምህር ፡ ለከ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ዘኢይምህሮሙ ፡ ኃጥዓን ፡ አወጽኦሙ ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወለእለ ፡ አፍቀርከዎሙ ፡ አመጽአሙ ፡፥ ኅቤሃ ። ወየዓርፉ ፡ ባቲ ። ወለ አለ ፡ ያፈቅርዋ ፡ አፈቅሮሙ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ይጸልዕዋ ፡ አጸልፆሙ ። ወእሜሩ ፡ እምድር ፡ ዝከሮሙ ። ወአጠፍዕ ፡ ዘርፆሙ ።
buried inside your hermitage of Tekuz, I will have mercy for you, and I will not have mercy for (the other) sinners: I will make them go out from there. And those whom I love, I will make them come there and they will rest there. And those who love the (hermitage), I will love them and I will hate those who hate it, and I will root out their memory from the earth and I will extinguish their offspring".
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተካየደቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ በ ፪ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ። እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ዘወሀበኒ ፡ ወልደየ ፡ እገብር ፡ ለከ ፥ ኅድግሰ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ኃጥዓነ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ አሥራተ ፡ ወሀበኒ ። ወናሁ ፡ አአርፈከ ፡ አምሕማም ፡ ቀሊል ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ሕማምከ ፡ ቀሊል ፡ ያፈድፍድ ፡ ለከ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ብዙኃ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ዘፃመወ ፡ በዓለም ፡ የሐዩ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ። ወበከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይቤ ፥ ዘፃመወ ፡ እምሉ ፥ ሐዋርያ ። እስመ ፡ ሕማምነ ፡ ዘለለ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ቅሊል ። ከብረ ፡ ወስብ ሐተ ። ብዙኃ ፡ አፈደፍደ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ለከ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ወሀበቶ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ አቡነ ። ወነገሮሙ ፡ ለደ ቁቁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡፥ እምቀዳሚ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደኃሪ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ልብከ ፡ ወበርሃ ፡ ገጽከ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኢት ሬአይዋኑ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ መላእከት ፡ አዕላፍ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ። ወምስለ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወሰማዕት ። ምስለ ፡ ደናግል ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ጌራን ። ወዘከመ ፡ መጽኡ ፥ ቫሎሙ ። ወኢያአመርከሙኑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ። ወዘከመ ፡ ይናዝዙኒ ። ወአ ንሰ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ወበርሃ ፡ ገጽየ ። እንከሰ ፡ አሐውር ፡
105 And after this, our Lady, the holy doubly Virgin Mary Mother of God, made the covenant with him, saying: "All the Covenant of Mercy that my Son gave me I make for you: for the righteous who have a little of good works it is certain! He also gave me the sinners (as) a tithe (Nm 18:26). And behold, I will make you rest from a slight suffering; and this slight suffering of yours will accumulate much glory for you, as the prophet David said: "He who labored in the world, will live forever' (Ps 48:10?); and as the apostle who labored more than all said: 'In fact our labor, which for every hour is light, will make much honor and glory accumulate for you' (2 Cor 4:17)". Jesus and Mary renew the Covenant of Mercy to the saint 106 And thus she gave our father the Covenant of Mercy with her beloved Son Jesus Christ. And, when he heard this, our father rejoiced and told his children everything that happened from the beginning to the end. And his children said to our father: "Why has your heart rejoiced and your face been illuminated?". And our father said to them: "Did you not see our Lady Mary, Mother of God, with her beloved Son Jesus Christ and with thousands of angels, with the prophets and the apostles and with the righteous and the martyrs, with the virgins and the pious monks, and how they all came? And did you not perceive all this, and how they comforted me? And as for me, for this, my heart rejoiced and my face was illuminated. And therefore I am going where those fathers of mine went". Pious death of Malke'a Krestos

107 And while he was saying this, a column of light rose from his bed to his tomb. And at that moment his soul went out from his body, at the hour of midnight of that day of the first Saturday. And the holy angels accompanied it, while they glorified him and chanted psalms before him and behind him. And all the saints, according to their category, made him ascend into the heaven of heavens and led him before the Holy Trinity. And the angels made that soul reign and magnified it in the heavenly Jerusalem. May he make us enter too! May the blessing of our father Malke'a Krestos be with our father Gabra Sellase and his beloved Walatta Sellase, and his father [empty space] and his mother [empty space], for ever and ever, amen! Funeral of Malke'a Krestos 108 And after his soul had gone out from his body, there was a great earthquake and the wild beasts and the birds cried out and saints came who were riding

the clouds and others who were riding the lions and others who were riding the elephants and others who were flying with wings like eagles, and some who were riding the leopards and some were going on foot, and they gathered where the body of our father Malke'a Krestos was, and they recited the psalms of David and the ritual of the deceased over his corpse. And they incensed with pure incense, as is due for the deceased, and they scattered on that place the dust of Wali of Abba Samuel as our Lady, the doubly Virgin Mary Mother of God, had promised him. Weeping of the disciples 109 And his children wept, saying: "Abba, abba, where are you going and to whom are you leaving us? Since you have been our father and you our mother and you our light and you our eye and you our treasure and you our nourishment until today, with the grace of the Holy Spirit. You have been our drink and you our garment, you our refuge and you our boast and you our glory and you our grace. You our good shepherd who protects us from the rapacious wolf; you the good shepherd who gives his life for his sheep, and you do not leave your sheep to the wolf, since you are not a mercenary shepherd, whose sheep are not his. "And the mercenary instead, who is not a shepherd and the sheep are not his, when he sees the wolf, he flees and abandons the sheep; the wolf comes and devours the sheep' (Jn 10:12)".

110 And again the people said to him: "You are our father: where have you gone? Our father, you have left us, you who had multiplied us with your prayer. In fact our city that had collapsed has risen from its fall and has been rebuilt with your hands after it had remained destroyed; and hunger and thirst were not found in your days. You have been our shield in the time of war; you have freed us from the powerful snare; you are the refuge of our soul and of our body; you are our refuge and you are the column of our faith that was planted in our land; you are our ladder that makes us ascend from the earth to heaven (Gen 28:12); you are our tower before our enemies (Ps 60:4). And now, abba, abba, to whom will you leave us?". And while they were saying this, men and women, old and young were weeping. And after this, the holy men, (the monks), wrapped our father Malke'a Krestos and buried him with songs and hymns in the tomb that our Lord and the mother of our Lord and our savior Jesus Christ had blessed for him. father on the 5th of Hamle and some make his commemoration on the 21st of Hamle with the feast of the birth of Eustathius our father and our teacher; and some make his commemoration on the 5th of Maskaram with the feast of the commemoration of our father Aron since on that day he had performed a miracle in the hermitage of Gesag. And the children of our father Malke'a Krestos sent a letter to the king. And the king, having learned that our father had died, was very saddened, since he loved him greatly. And for the commemoration of our father, he ordered (that) fattened oxen and sheep and everything that was necessary for the feast (be prepared). And furthermore his children sent (the news) to all the hermitages. And all the brother monks, having heard that he had died, wept, saying: "Abba, abba, boast of the monks and star of the hermitages and torch of Ethiopia and preacher of the gospel of the kingdom and word of the faith: why have you lain down and fallen asleep like a man in the dust?". It is finished, in the peace of the Lord. May the blessing and the help of our father Malke'a Krestos be with our father Gabra Sellase and his beloved Walatta Sellase and his father [empty space] and his mother [empty space], for ever and ever, amen! Effigy of Malke'a Krestos 1 In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, who are one God, who is one in their power, and threefold is their person; and in the name of Mary the Virgin, I begin the word of her poem: and may the friends of Malke'a Krestos help me, and may the angels reveal what is hidden from me of the mystery.
ሰላም ፡ ለዝከረ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘተሰብከ ። በልደተ ፡ ጸጋ ፥ ሥላሴ ፡ አመ ፡ ወለደከ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኢትኅዝን ፡ እንተ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ኪያከ ። እምግብር ፡ ሰይጣናዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ በጥበብ ፡ አምሠጥከ ። ወምስለ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ በተፋቅሮ ፡ ዘመነ ፡ ፈጸምከ ።
2 Hail to the mention of your name which was preached with Christ, when he generated you with the birth of the grace of the Trinity; Malke'a Krestos, do not be saddened, it was not yours (the iniquity) since from the satanic work with wisdom you escaped, and with the Creator, in love you finished the time.
ሰላም ፡ ለስእርትከ ፡ ወለርእስከ ፡ ዘቆብዕ ። እንተ ፡ መነነ ፡ ተጸፍሮ ፡ ወተቀብዖ ፡ ቅብዕ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ መዋዔ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ዓለሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ። መኑ ፡ ከማከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ በዘመንነ ፡ እምሰብእ ። ለዓለም ፡ እንተ ፡ ሞዖ ፡ በኅዳጥ ፡ መዊዕ ።
3 Hail to your hair, and to your head with the monastic cap, which despised the styling and the anointing of the ointment, Malke'a Krestos, victor of the love of the world of men: who is among men, like you, O lord, in our time, who conquered the world with a small victory?


[p. 372] {#po.51.p372}
ሰላም ፡ ለገጽከ ፡ ሳሌዳ ፡ ሞገስ ፡ ወግርማ ። ወለቀራንብቲከ ፡ ፪ ኤ ፡ ንዋመ ፡ ሐኪት ፡ እለ ፡ ኢኖማ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ እንተ ፡ አዕረፍከ ፡ እምነ ፡ ፃማ ። ማርያም ፡ እግዝእትከ ፡፥ ለእመ ፡ ትጹውዕ ፡ ስማ ። ለሐውፆትከ ፡ ለከ ፡ ትወርድ ፡ እምራማ ።
4 Hail to your face, tablet of grace and majesty, and to your two eyelids that did not sleep the sleep of laziness; Malke'a Krestos, righteous, who rested from fatigue; you invoked the name of the mother, Mary, your lady, she descended from Rama to pay attention to you.
ሰላም ፡ ለአአይንቲከ ፡ እለ ፡ ነጸራ ፥ ምሥጢራተ ። ወለአዕዛኒከ ፡ ሰላም ፡ እለ ፡ አፅምዓ ፡ ቃላተ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ፈጻሜ ፡ ሕንፃ ፡ እንጦኒ ፡ ሥርዓተ ። ለወልድከ ፡ ፈጻሜ ፡ አከይ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይስማዕ ፡ ጸሎተ ። በመዓልት ፡ ወበሌሊት ፡ ግበር ፡ ሊተ ።
5 Hail to your eyes that contemplated the mysteries, and to your ears hail, that listened to the words, Malke'a Krestos, perfecter of the building of the rule of Anthony who listens to the prayer for your son, doer of evil, work for me by day and by night.
ሰላም ፡ ለመላትሂከ ፡ ወለአዕናፊከ ፡ መሳከው ። እንተ ፡ ያበውዖ!ን ፡ ፄናሁ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። መልከዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ተላዊ ፡ መምሕራን ፡ አበው ። ኢትግዝመኒ ፡ በኢድልወትየ ፡ ምስለ ፥ ኃጥዓን ፡ ዕደው ። አምጣነ ፡ በፈትሕ ፡ አነ ፡ ለሞት ፡ ድልው ።
6 Hail to your cheeks and to your nostrils, the windows that let in the perfume of the living God, Malke'a Krestos, disciple follower of the father teachers: do not cut me off for my unworthiness together with the sinners, until in justice I will be ready for death.
ሰላም ፡ ለከናፍሪከ ፡ ዘኢተናገራ ፡ ሕሳዌ ። ወለአፉከ ፡ ጸዋዔ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ሰማያዌ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ኩኖ ፡ ለማኅበርከ ፡ ኖላዌ ። አምአባግዒከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይምሥጥ ፡ አርዌ ። በከመ ፥ መሠጣ ፡ ሰሐዋ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ጐህላዌ ።
7 Hail to your lips that did not speak falsehoods, and to your mouth, invoker of the heavenly God: Malke'a Krestos, be shepherd of your community, so that the wild beast does not rob any of your sheep, just as, being a defrauder, it robbed Eve.
ሰላም ፡ ለአስናኒከ ፡ ፀዓዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ በረድ ። ወሰኩዕ ፡ ከመ ፡ መረግድ ። መልክዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዘትሰብከ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ በአውድ ። ተዘከረነ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ አመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ፍዳ ፡ መርዕድ ። እስመ ፡ አብ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ውሉድ ።
8 Hail to your teeth, white as hail and set like emeralds, Malke'a Krestos, who in the assembly preached the faith of the Trinity remember us in your prayer, on the day of the tremendous condemnation, since you are the father and we the children.
ሰላም ፡ ለልሳንከ ፡ ወሀቤ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ በበሰዓቱ ። ወለቃልከ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ምስለ ፡ እሳታውያን ፡ ለለፀዓቱ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ሰአሎ ፡ በእንተ ፥ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲቱ ። ለዘፈጠረከ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ጂቱ ።
9 Hail to your tongue, bestower of praises at all hours, and to your voice, hail, at every exit of it with the fiery (angels), Malke'a Krestos, pray through Mary, his mother, the God of the seven heavens who created you, so that we do not remain uselessly loving the vain world.
ፍቁራነ ፡ ከንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኢንትርፍ ፡ በከንቱ ። ሰላም ፡ ለጉርዔከ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ብሉያት ፡ ወሐዲሳት ። ወለከሣድከ ፡ ሥርግው ፡ <በ»አስኬማ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ መላእክት ። መልከዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ምላዕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤትከ ፡ ሠናይት ። በረከተከ ፡ መንፈሳዌ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማየ ፡ ፅኑዕ ፡ ከረምት ። ዘነሣእከ ፡ እምፍርፋሩ ፡ ለአምላከ ፡ ብሁት ።
10 Hail to your throat, way of the old things and of the new (Mt 13:52), and to your neck, adorned with the scapular of the holy angels, Malke'a Krestos, fill your beautiful house with your spiritual blessing like the safe water of the rainy season, which you took from the crumbs (Mt 15:27) of the powerful God


[p. 374] {#po.51.p374}
ሰላም ፡ ለ-ረእ»ቴመት(ት)ከ ፡ ፀዋሬ ፡ አርዑት ፡ ሠናይ ። ወለዘባንከ ፡ ሰላም ፡ በልብሰ ፥ ተከህኖ ፡ ርሠይ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ከቡር ፡ እምአዝማዲከ ፡ ኅሩይ ። ለወልድከ ፡ እስኩ ፡ ኢያይብስኒ ፡ ጌጋይ ። ያጥለለኒ ፡ አባ ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ማይ ።
11 Hail to your forearm bearer of the sweet yoke (Mt 11:30), and to your back, hail, clothed with the priestly vestment Malke'a Krestos glorious, chosen by your people: or therefore, so that sin does not make me dry, your son, your prayer, father, may irrigate me like water.
፲2 ሰላም ፡ ለአንግድአከ ፡ ወለኅፅንከ ፡ ምንምፅላሉ ። ለቤትከ ፡ ምዕረፈ ፡ ኩሉ ። መልክዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ለከርስቶስ ፡ አምሳሉ ። ሰአል ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሰአሉ ።
12 Hail to your chest and to your breast, shadowy place of your house, rest of all, Malke'a Krestos, righteous, similar to Christ, ask for us, as he said: "Ask and seek" (Mt 7:7), so that you may find the Lord with his word.
ወኅሠ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ትርከቡ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ በቃሉ ። ሰላም ፡ ለአዕዳዊከ ፡ ወለመዛርኢከ ፡ ፪ ኤ ። ለአጽምዖ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘሰ(ሰንፋህከ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ጉባዔ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ነሳሒ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በበሱባኤ ። ኃሥኩከ ፡ አባ ፡፥ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትዓፀው ፡ ጐርኤ ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ አፍ ፡ ኪያከ ፡ ረዳኤ ።
13 Hail to your hands and to your two arms, to listen to the new gospel that you expounded before the assembly, Malke'a Krestos, penitent man, every week I have sought you, abba, before the throat closed and before your helping mouth fell silent.
ሰላም ፡ ለኩርናዕከ ፡ ወለመዛርኢከ ፡ መጣኒ ። ለሐኒፀ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ለድንግል ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘይኒ ። መልከዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ተላዊ ፡ አሠረ ፡ እንጦኒ ፡ ወአርስኒ ። በሳምናዊ ፡ ዘመንነ ፡ አልቦ ፡ መናኒ ። እንበሌከ ፡ አባ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ወጣኒ ።
14 Hail to your elbows and to your arms, surveyor for the building of the house of the Virgin in the best (place), Malke'a Krestos, follower of the footsteps of Anthony and of Arsenius in our eighth era there is no despiser (of the world), except you, abba, there is no initiator
ሰላም ፡ ለ(ርንአራኅከ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ በረከት ፡ ወሀቢ ። ወለአፃብዒከ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ሰላም ፡ አፅናረ" ፡፥ አናግድ ፡ ሐፃቢ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ ዘአልብከ ፡ ተሐዛቢ ። ለነፍስየ ፥ በላ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ተናባቢ ። ወእምተራኅርፕ ፡ ለአምላከ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ቅረቢ ።
15 Hail to your palms, givers of the bread of blessing, and hail, hail to your fingers, that washed the nails of the pilgrims, Malke'a Krestos, who have no fear for justice, say to my soul, O lord who speak of peace: "Approach God with mercy".
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለአፃብዒከ ፡ ብሩሃን ። ወለገቦከ ፡ እዝ ፡ በሠቀ ፡ ሐሂን ። መልክዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ጸዋዔ ፡ ስመ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ጎራን ። አአባ ፡ ለማዕዜ(ከ ፡ ኪዳን ። እ-ጐዘ ፡ በመሥገርት ፡ ያሠግረኒ ፥ ጸላዒ ፡ ሰይጣን ።
16 Hail, I say, to your shining fingers, which held the iron hairshirt to your chest, Malke'a Krestos, invoker of the name of the good Trinity: O abba, when will the covenant [be fulfilled], while the enemy Satan captures me with his snare?
ሰላም ፡ ለከርሥከ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ መባልዕት ፡ ዘሞአ ። ወለልብከ ፡ ሰላም ፡ አሚነ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ርቱዓ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ጻድቀ ፡፥ መምሕረ ፡ ውሣጤ ፡ ወአፍአ ። እምወይነ ፡ ጌጋይከ ፡ ታረዊ ፡ ፅሙዓ ። ወለርጥባን ፡ ታፀግብ ፥ መበልዓ ።
17 Hail to your belly, which overcame the love of food, and hail to your heart, upright in the faith of the Trinity, Malke'a Krestos, righteous teacher, inside and out; with the wine of your pilgrimage you made the thirsty drink and satisfied the hungry with food.


[p. 376] {#po.51.p376}
ሰላም ፡ ለልብከ ፡ <ለ»ተዘከሮ ፡ ሞት ፡ መጽሔቱ ። ወለሕሊናከ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ተስፋ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ትርሚቱ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ አውርድ ፡ መና ፡ ጸጋከ ፡ ለለዕለቱ ። ውሉድከ ፡ አባ ፡ ኢንትኃፈር ፡ ቦቱ ። ወኢይበሱኒ ፡ ፀር ፡ ሕስዋን ፡ እሙንቱ ።
18 Hail to your heart, a mirror for remembering death, and hail to your mind, adorned with the hope of the resurrection, Malke'a Krestos; send down every day the manna of your grace, [so that] we your children, O father, will not be shamed by it, and those lying enemies will not speak to me.
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለልያቲከ ፡ መንታ ። ወለአማዑቲከ ፡ እብል ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንዋየ ፡፥ ውሥጥከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ኃተታ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ለአሚነ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ መሠረታ ። ከልለኒ ፡ አባ ፡ በጸጋከ ፡ ወልታ ። ዓለም ፡ ኢትቅትለኒ ፡ በፈቲው ፡ ቀሥታ ።
19 Hail, I say, to your twin kidneys and to your bowels, I say, with your internal organs, at the time of trial, Malke'a Krestos, righteous one, foundation of the faith of Peter (Lk 22:32); protect me, father, with your grace, like a shield, [so that] the world may not kill me with greed, the cause of condemnation.
ሰላም ፡ ለኅንብርትከ ፡ ወለሐቋከ ፡ ዕሱር ። በሠቀ ፡ ሐዩን ፡ ቄሪር ። መናኔ ፡ ዓለማት ፡ አባ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ መምሕር ። ለነፍስየ ፡ ኩና ፥ አግዚኦ ፡ መምሕር ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወፍቅር ።
20 Hail to your navel and to your loins, girded with a hairshirt of cold iron, despiser of the ages, abba Malke'a Krestos, teacher: O lord, be peace and love, a teacher for my soul, a word in the likeness of the arcane mystery.
ከመ ፡ ምሳሌሁ ፡ ለሥውር ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ ነገር ። ሰላም ፡ ለአዮያጺከ ፡ ወለአብራኪከ ፡ ሰጋዲ ። ለሕያው ፡ ወላዲ ፡ ወለሕያው ፡ ተወላዲ ። መልከዓ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አብ ፡ ለሐውፆ ፡ ቤትከ ፡ ዘኢትጐንዲ ። አትርፍ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ እምጸጋከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትንዲ ። ከመ ፡ አመ ፡ ለውሉዱ ፡ ገዋየ ፡ ያተርፍ ፡ ነጋዲ ።
21 Hail to your legs and to your knees, worshippers of the living Begetter and the living Begotten, Malke'a Krestos, father, who do not delay in visiting your house; leave for me, O lord, something of your grace so as not to impoverish yourself, as when a merchant earns something for his children.
ሰላም ፡ ለአዕጋሪከ ፥ በመንኖ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘጌሣ ። ፍኖተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃሥሣ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ጸሊ ፡ ለሥጋየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነፍሳ ። ይጹውወኒ ፡ መንገሌሆሙ ፡ እምእደ ፡ ጸላዒ ፡ አንበሳ ። ሕገጋተ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ኢዮአብ ፡ ወካልዕ ፡ አቢሳ ።
22 Hail to your feet, which, despising the world, set out quickly seeking the way of the hermitage, Malke'a Krestos; pray for my body for the sake of my soul, may the laws of God take me for them from the hand of the enemy lion, (like) Joab and the other Abishai.
ሰላም ፡ ለሰከዋኒከ”" ፡ አለ ፡ ደከማ ፡ በተሰዶ ። ምስለ ፡ መከየድከ ፡ በተዓንግዶ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ከብረ ፡ ዘረከብከ ፡ በተዋርዶ ። ብከ ፡ ዘተማኅፀነ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ መፍቅዶ ። ከመ ፡ ወላዲ ፡ ያስተናስዕ ፡ እንተ ፡ ደከመ ፡ ወልዶ ።
23 Hail to the soles of your feet that labored in exile, with your walking in pilgrimage, Malke'a Krestos, who found glory in submission; whoever trusts in you fulfills his desire, as a parent refreshes his child who is tired.
ሰላም ፡ ለአፃብዒከ ፡ ወለአጽፋሪከ ፡ ኩላ(ም ። ጽጌያተ ፡ እለ ፡፥ ይመስላ ። መልክዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ አፅንዕ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ወመሐላ ። አጽራርየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንስርት ፡ ወአነ ፡ ገደላ ። እምኩሉ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ አድኅኒዮ ፡ በላ ።
24 Hail to your fingers and to all your nails, which are like flowers, Malke'a Krestos; strengthen the covenant and the oath; my enemies are like the eagles and I the corpse (Mt 24:28; Lk 17:31), tell Mary to save him from all.


[p. 378] {#po.51.p378}
ሰላም ፡ ለቆምከ ፡ ዘቆመ ፡ አዳም ፡ ምድራዊ ። ወለመልከዕከ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ አምላክከ ፡ ሰማያዊ ። ለባርኮትየ ፡ ነዓ ፡ መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አምላካዊ ። ለወልድከ ፡ አባ ፡ በሰዓተ ፡ ፅምዑ ፡ ፃማዊ ። እስመ ፡ ፅዋዑ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ያረዊ ።
25 Hail to your stature, which (is) the stature of the earthly Adam, and to your figure still, the figure (Gen 1:26) of the heavenly God; behold Malke'a Krestos with the divine, for my blessing, father, in the hour of his painful thirst for your son, for the cup of the father quenches thirst.
ሰላም ፡ ለፀዓተ ፡ ንፍስከ ፡ እምግንዘተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡፥ ግሥሥ ። ወለበድነ ፡ ሥጋከ ፥ ግኑነዝ ፡ በግንዘተ ፥ ሕያው ፡ ከርስቶስ ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ሰአል ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወነፍስ ። አኮኑ ፡ ያበጽሐኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሞት ፡ ጥሉል ፡ በለስ ። ማኅደረ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ስስዕተ ፡ ዘዚአየ ፡ ከርሥ ።
26 Hail to the departure of your soul from the wrapping of the tangible body, and to the corpse of your body wrapped in the shroud of the living Christ, Malke'a Krestos; ask for the mercy of body and soul: shall the flourishing fig tree (Gen 3), dwelling of Satan, that is, my greedy belly, lead me to death?
ሰላም ፡ ለመቃብሪከ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትትሔደሱ ፡ በኅቤሃ ። ከመ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ሰከብከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ትትነሥኡ ፡ እምኔሃ ። መልክዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ለአግዝእትከ ፡ በቅድሜሃ ። አማኅፀንኩከ ፡ አባ ፡ ትፀግወኒ ፡ አምኃ ። ወፍሥ-ሕርየ ፡ ዚአየ ፡ ግበር ፡ ዘአንተ ፡ ፍሥሐ ።
27 Hail to your tomb, until you are renewed beside it, as you lay in it, from it you will rise, Malke'a Krestos, my father; before your lady, I entrust myself to you, abba, give me a gift, and make my happiness, you who are happiness.
ስብሐት ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ ለልብየ ፡ ዘአዳም ። ወለማርያም ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘረረ<ድ>አተኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፍጽም ። ስብሐት :» ስብሐት ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ዘኢተቀየሞ ። ከመ ፡ ነገርከኒ ፡ ለመልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ ሰላሞ ። ለአፄንዎ ፡ ኅበኒ ፡ ፍቅረከ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ አበሞ ።
28 Glory to the Lord who gladdens my heart, and glory to Mary who helps me until the end, glory, glory, Christ, who does not harbor resentment, so that he may narrate to me the peace of Malke'a Krestos; to inhale the perfume, give me your love, give me the aroma.
አባ ፡ አባ ፡ አባ ፡ ከቡር ። መልከዓ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፡ መምሕር ። አድኅነኒ ፡ አምዓይነ ፡ ፀር ፡ ወእምዓይነ ፡ ሞት ፡ መሪር ። እስመ ፣፡ ርኅሩኃ ፡ ልብ ፡ አንተ ፡ ለሉ ፡ ፍጡር ። ነዓ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ኅርበ ፡ ገብርከ ፡ ፍቁር =>
29 Abba, abba, glorious abba, Malke'a Krestos, teacher, save me from the eye of the enemy and from the eye of bitter death, for you are a merciful heart for all creatures. Come, come, come from [your beloved servant].
